============================================================
INTELLISHARE - PROJECT SOURCE DOCUMENTATION
============================================================
Project: IntelliShare - Secure and Intelligent File Sharing System
Student: Anjali Agrahari
Degree: Master of Computer Applications (MCA)
Framework: Django / Python
Frontend: HTML, CSS, JavaScript, Bootstrap
Database: PostgreSQL (SQLite was also used during development)

PURPOSE
This document contains a project overview, source-file inventory,
and the contents of selected source and configuration files. It is
intended to support technical review and preparation of an academic
project report.

IMPORTANT
- This export does not prove that every feature works correctly.
- The file inventory may include files that are not used at runtime.
- Environment files, databases, uploaded media, and common private-key
  files are excluded.
- Review this document before sharing it. Do not share passwords,
  secret keys, tokens, personal data, or private configuration values.

======================================================================
FILE: .github\workflows\django.yml
======================================================================
name: Django CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "main" ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ "main" ]

jobs:
  build:

    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      max-parallel: 4
      matrix:
        python-version: [3.7, 3.8, 3.9]

    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      uses: actions/setup-python@v3
      with:
        python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
    - name: Install Dependencies
      run: |
        python -m pip install --upgrade pip
        pip install -r requirements.txt
    - name: Run Tests
      run: |
        python manage.py test

======================================================================
FILE: accounts\antivirus\clamav.py
======================================================================
import subprocess
import os
import shutil

# Automatically find ClamAV from system PATH
CLAMSCAN_PATH = shutil.which("clamscan")


def scan_file(file_path):

    # Check file exists
    if not os.path.exists(file_path):
        return False, "File to scan does not exist."

    # Check ClamAV exists
    if not CLAMSCAN_PATH:
        return False, "ClamAV scanner is not installed."

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [
                CLAMSCAN_PATH,
                "--no-summary",
                file_path
            ],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=30
        )

        print("ClamAV Path:", CLAMSCAN_PATH)
        print("ClamAV Return Code:", result.returncode)
        print("ClamAV Output:", result.stdout)
        print("ClamAV Error:", result.stderr)

        # Return code 0 = clean
        if result.returncode == 0:
            return True, "File is clean."

        # Return code 1 = malware found
        elif result.returncode == 1:
            return False, "Malware detected in the uploaded file."

        # Return code 2 or anything else = scanner problem
        else:
            error_message = result.stderr or result.stdout
            return False, f"ClamAV scanning failed: {error_message}"

    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return False, "Malware scan timed out. Upload blocked."

    except Exception as e:
        print("ClamAV Exception:", str(e))
        return False, f"Unable to scan the file: {str(e)}"

FILE: accounts\audit.py

from .models import AuditLog


def get_client_ip(request):
    if not request:
        return None
    x_forwarded_for = request.META.get("HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR")
    if x_forwarded_for:
        ip = x_forwarded_for.split(",")[0].strip()
    else:
        ip = request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR")
    return ip


def parse_device_information(user_agent_str):
    if not user_agent_str:
        return {"browser": "Unknown", "os": "Unknown"}

    ua = user_agent_str.lower()
    os_name = "Other"
    if "windows" in ua:
        os_name = "Windows"
    elif "macintosh" in ua or "mac os" in ua:
        os_name = "macOS"
    elif "linux" in ua:
        os_name = "Linux"
    elif "android" in ua:
        os_name = "Android"
    elif "iphone" in ua or "ipad" in ua:
        os_name = "iOS"

    browser_name = "Other"
    if "edg" in ua:
        browser_name = "Edge"
    elif "chrome" in ua:
        browser_name = "Chrome"
    elif "firefox" in ua:
        browser_name = "Firefox"
    elif "safari" in ua:
        browser_name = "Safari"
    elif "postman" in ua:
        browser_name = "Postman"
    elif "python" in ua or "requests" in ua:
        browser_name = "Python Client"

    return {"browser": browser_name, "os": os_name, "raw": user_agent_str[:200]}


def infer_event_type(action):
    act = (action or "").strip().upper().replace("-", "_").replace(" ", "_")

    # Security events must be checked first.
    if any(k in act for k in [
        "MALWARE", "INTEGRITY", "TAMPER", "SUSPICIOUS",
        "BRUTE", "ABUSE", "UNAUTHORIZED", "SECURITY_INCIDENT",
    ]):
        return "SECURITY"

    # Authentication events.
    if any(k in act for k in [
        "LOGIN", "LOGOUT", "OTP", "AUTH", "PASSWORD",
        "SIGNIN", "SIGNUP", "ACTIVAT", "REGISTER",
    ]):
        return "AUTHENTICATION"

    # Sharing and access-control events.
    if any(k in act for k in [
        "SHARE", "ACCESS_REQUEST", "ACCESS_DENIED",
        "ACCESS_GRANTED", "REVOK", "EXPIRED",
    ]):
        return "SHARING"

    # File and folder operations.
    if any(k in act for k in [
        "UPLOAD", "DOWNLOAD", "DELETE", "FILE",
        "ENCRYPT", "DECRYPT", "FOLDER", "INTEGRITY",
    ]):
        return "FILE_ACTIVITY"

    # Do not label unknown events as security incidents.
    return "OTHER"



def create_audit_log(
    user=None,
    action="",
    description="",
    request=None,
    event_type=None,
    success=True,
    reason="",
    metadata=None,
    device_information=None,
    risk_level="LOW",
    risk_score=0,
    suspicious=False,
    trigger_evaluation=True,
):
    ip_address = None
    user_agent = None

    if request:
        ip_address = get_client_ip(request)
        user_agent = request.META.get("HTTP_USER_AGENT")

    if device_information is None:
        device_information = parse_device_information(user_agent)

    if metadata is None:
        metadata = {}

    if not event_type:
        event_type = infer_event_type(action)

    audit_log = AuditLog.objects.create(
        user=user if (user and user.is_authenticated) else None,
        action=action,
        description=description,
        ip_address=ip_address,
        user_agent=user_agent,
        metadata=metadata,
        event_type=event_type,
        success=success,
        reason=reason,
        device_information=device_information,
        risk_level=risk_level,
        risk_score=risk_score,
        suspicious=suspicious,
    )

    if trigger_evaluation:
        try:
            from security_ai.engine import evaluate_security_event
            evaluate_security_event(audit_log, request=request)
        except Exception as e:
            # Safe fallback: never disrupt transaction or normal user flow
            print(f"[Audit] Warning: Anomaly detection evaluation failed: {e}")

    return audit_log

FILE: accounts\email_utils.py
======================================================================
from threading import Thread
from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.conf import settings


def send_email_async(subject, message, recipient):
    """
    Send email in a background thread.
    """

    def _send():
        send_mail(
            subject=subject,
            message=message,
            from_email=settings.EMAIL_HOST_USER,
            recipient_list=[recipient],
            fail_silently=False,
        )

    Thread(target=_send, daemon=True).start()

from django import forms
from django.contrib.auth.forms import PasswordChangeForm
from .models import User


class ProfileUpdateForm(forms.ModelForm):
    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = ["first_name", "last_name", "email"]

        widgets = {
            "first_name": forms.TextInput(attrs={
                "class": "form-control",
                "placeholder": "First Name"
            }),
            "last_name": forms.TextInput(attrs={
                "class": "form-control",
                "placeholder": "Last Name"
            }),
            "email": forms.EmailInput(attrs={
                "class": "form-control",
                "placeholder": "Email Address"
            }),
        }

FILE: accounts\managers.py
======================================================================
from django.contrib.auth.base_user import BaseUserManager


class CustomUserManager(BaseUserManager):

    def create_user(self, email, password=None, **extra_fields):

        if not email:
            raise ValueError("Email is required")

        email = self.normalize_email(email)

        user = self.model(email=email, **extra_fields)

        user.set_password(password)

        user.save(using=self._db)

        return user


    def create_superuser(self, email, password=None, **extra_fields):

        extra_fields.setdefault("is_staff", True)
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_superuser", True)
        extra_fields.setdefault("is_active", True)

        return self.create_user(email, password, **extra_fields)

======================================================================
FILE: accounts\models.py
======================================================================
import uuid
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils import timezone
from .managers import CustomUserManager
from datetime import timedelta
import hashlib


class User(AbstractUser):

    username = None
    full_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    email = models.EmailField(unique=True)
    phone_number = models.CharField(max_length=15, blank=True)
    profile_picture = models.ImageField(
        upload_to='profiles/',
        blank=True,
        null=True
    )
    two_factor_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    USERNAME_FIELD = "email"
    REQUIRED_FIELDS = []

    objects = CustomUserManager()

    def __str__(self):
        return self.email
class Folder(models.Model):
        owner = models.ForeignKey(
            settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
            on_delete=models.CASCADE,
            related_name='folders'
        )
        name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
        parent = models.ForeignKey(
            'self',
            on_delete=models.CASCADE,
            null=True, blank=True,
            related_name='subfolders'
        )
        created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

        def __str__(self):
            return self.name


class File(models.Model):

    owner = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='files'
    )

    folder = models.ForeignKey(
        Folder,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='files'
    )

    file_name = models.CharField(max_length=255)

    file = models.FileField(
    upload_to='encrypted_files/',
    max_length=500
)

    file_size = models.BigIntegerField(default=0)

    # RSA encrypted AES key
    encrypted_key = models.BinaryField()

    # AES-GCM nonce
    

    is_encrypted = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    sha256_hash = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, default="")

    ENCRYPTION_CHOICES = (
        ('AES256', 'AES-256'),
        ('Dual', 'Dual Layer(AES + RSA)'),
    )
    nonce = models.BinaryField(null=True, blank=True)
    encryption_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ENCRYPTION_CHOICES,
        default='AES256'
    )

    def __str__(self):
        return self.file_name

class SharedFile(models.Model):
        PERMISSION_CHOICES = [
            ('view', 'View'),
            ('download', 'Download'),
        ]

        file = models.ForeignKey(File, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='shares')
        shared_by = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,on_delete=models.CASCADE,related_name='shared_files_sent')
        shared_with = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
            on_delete=models.CASCADE,
            null=True, blank=True,       # null = public link share
            related_name='shared_files_received'
        )
        receiver_email = models.EmailField(null=True,blank=True)
        permission = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PERMISSION_CHOICES, default='view')
        share_token = models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True)   # for shareable link
        share_password = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True)  # store hashed
        expires_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
        shared_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
        max_downloads = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=1)
        current_downloads = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
        is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

class Notification(models.Model):

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="notification"
    )

    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)

    message = models.TextField()

    is_read = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title
    

class EmailVerificationToken(models.Model):
        user=models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='verification_tokens')
        tokens=models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4,unique=True)
        expires_at=models.DateTimeField()
        is_used=models.BooleanField(default=False)
        created_at=models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
        def is_expired(self):
            return timezone.now() > self.expires_at
        def __str__(self):
            return f"Verification token for {self.user.email}"


class EncryptedFile(models.Model):
    ENCRYPTION_CHOICES = [
        ("AES", "AES-256"),
        ("Dual", "Dual Layer Encryption"),
    ]
    owner=models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    
    original_fileName=models.CharField(max_length=255)
    encrypted_file=models.FileField(upload_to='encrypted_files/')
    encryption_key=models.BinaryField()
    encrypted_key = models.BinaryField(null=True,blank=True)
    share_token=models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4, unique=True,editable=False)
    expires_at=models.DateTimeField()
    created_at=models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    is_active=models.BooleanField(default=True)  
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.original_fileName   


class SecureShareLink(models.Model):
    file=models.ForeignKey(File,on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="share_links")
    token=models.UUIDField(default=uuid.uuid4,unique=True,editable=False)
    created_by=models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    password="[REDACTED]"
    expires_at=models.DateTimeField(null=True,blank=True)
    created_at=models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    is_active=models.BooleanField(default=True)
    
    def __self__(self):    
        return str(self.token)    
    
class Downloading(models.Model):
    share = models.ForeignKey(
        SecureShareLink,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE
    )

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True
    )

    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField()

    downloaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True) 
    
class LoginOTP(models.Model):
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="login_otps"
    )

    otp = models.CharField(max_length=6)

    expires_at = models.DateTimeField()

    is_verified = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.expires_at:
            self.expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=5)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def is_expired(self):
        return timezone.now() > self.expires_at

    def __str__(self):
        return f"OTP for {self.user.email}" 
    
class AccessRequest(models.Model):
    STATUS_CHOICE=[("Pending","Pending"),("Approved","Approved")]
    shared_file=models.ForeignKey("SharedFile",on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="access_requests")
    requested_by=models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    status=models.CharField(max_length=20,choices=STATUS_CHOICE,default="Pending",db_index=True)
    requested_at=models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    class Meta:
        unique_together=("shared_file","requested_by")
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.requested_by}-{self.shared_file.file.file_name}"

    
    
class AuditLog(models.Model):

    ACTION_CHOICES = [

    ("LOGIN", "Login"),
    ("LOGIN_FAILED", "Login Failed"),
    ("LOGOUT", "Logout"),

    ("OTP_VERIFIED", "OTP Verified"),
    ("OTP_FAILED", "OTP Failed"),

    ("FILE_UPLOAD", "File Upload"),
    ("FILE_DOWNLOAD", "File Download"),
    ("FILE_DELETE", "File Delete"),

    ("FILE_SHARE", "File Share"),

    ("SHARE_LINK_CREATED", "Share Link Created"),
    ("SHARE_LINK_REVOKED", "Share Link Revoked"),

    (
        "SHARE_PASSWORD_FAILED",
        "Share Password Failed"
    ),

    (
        "EXPIRED_LINK_ACCESS",
        "Expired Link Access"
    ),

    (
        "REVOKED_LINK_ACCESS",
        "Revoked Link Access"
    ),

    ("ACCESS_REQUEST", "Access Request"),
    ("ACCESS_APPROVED", "Access Approved"),
    ("ACCESS_DENIED", "Access Denied"),

    (
        "MALWARE_DETECTED",
        "Malware Detected"
    ),

    (
        "INTEGRITY_FAILURE",
        "Integrity Verification Failure"
    ),

    ("PASSWORD_CHANGE", "Password Change"),

    ("2FA_ENABLED", "2FA Enabled"),
    ("2FA_DISABLED", "2FA Disabled"),
]

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="audit_logs"
    )

    action = models.CharField(
        max_length=50,
        choices=ACTION_CHOICES
    )

    reason = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        default=""
    )

    description = models.TextField()

    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(
        null=True,
        blank=True
    )

    user_agent = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        null=True
    )

    metadata = models.JSONField(
        default=dict,
        blank=True
    )

    risk_level = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        default="LOW"
    )

    risk_score = models.SmallIntegerField(
        default=0
    )

    success = models.BooleanField(
        default=True
    )

    suspicious = models.BooleanField(
        default=False
    )

    device_information = models.JSONField(
        default=dict,
        blank=True
    )

    event_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=50,
        default="AUTHENTICATION"
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user} - {self.action} - {self.created_at}"

              <i class="bi bi-clock-history text-info me-2"></i>Audit Trail Log
            </h5>

<!-- Interactive Real-Time Search & Filter Script -->
<script>
  function filterAuditLogs() {
    const query = document.getElementById('vs-log-search').value.toLowerCase();
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#vs-audit-table tbody tr');

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const text = row.textContent.toLowerCase();
      row.style.display = text.includes(query) ? '' : 'none';
    });
  }

  function filterByCategory(cat, btn) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.vs-filter-pill').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');

    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#vs-audit-table tbody tr');
    if (cat === 'ALL') {
      rows.forEach(r => r.style.display = '');
      return;
    }

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const text = row.textContent.toUpperCase();
      row.style.display = text.includes(cat) ? '' : 'none';
    });
  }
</script>

{% endblock %}

<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    if (typeof AOS !== 'undefined') {
      AOS.init({ duration: 600, once: true });
    }
  });

  /* Search Filter Helper */
  function filterDashboardTable(query) {
    const term = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('table tbody tr');
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const text = row.textContent.toLowerCase();
      if (!term || text.includes(term)) {
        row.style.display = '';
      } else {
        row.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });
  }
</script>

<!-- Interactive Logic for Hero Simulator & Audit Feed -->
<script>
  function toggleHeroView(view) {
    const simView = document.getElementById('hero-view-sim');
    const svgView = document.getElementById('hero-view-svg');
    const simBtn = document.getElementById('tab-sim-btn');
    const svgBtn = document.getElementById('tab-svg-btn');

    if (view === 'sim') {
      simView.style.display = 'block';
      svgView.style.display = 'none';
      simBtn.classList.add('active');
      svgBtn.classList.remove('active');
    } else {
      simView.style.display = 'none';
      svgView.style.display = 'block';
      svgBtn.classList.add('active');
      simBtn.classList.remove('active');
    }
  }

  function runEncryptionSim() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('sim-file-select').value;
    const hashElem = document.getElementById('sim-hash');
    const urlElem = document.getElementById('sim-output-url');

    const mockHashes = {
      financial: '8f9b2c01a4e29d71...',
      credentials: '3e41b99c001f7a22...',
      patient: '7c8801d9f2231b40...'
    };

    const mockUrls = {
      financial: 'https://vaultshare.io/s/fin-99a1#k=aes256',
      credentials: 'https://vaultshare.io/s/sec-44x2#k=rsa2048',
      patient: 'https://vaultshare.io/s/med-11z8#k=gcm256'
    };

    hashElem.textContent = mockHashes[sel] || '8f9b2c01a...e4d9';
    urlElem.textContent = mockUrls[sel] || 'https://vaultshare.io/s/v8a9-x719#k=992f8a';
  }

  function copySimUrl() {
    const text = document.getElementById('sim-output-url').textContent;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
      const btn = document.getElementById('sim-copy-btn');
      btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-check2 me-1"></i>Copied';
      setTimeout(() => {
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-clipboard me-1"></i>Copy';
      }, 2000);
    });
  }
</script>

{% endblock %}

                  <i class="bi bi-eye" id="eye-icon"></i>
                </button>
              </div>

              <!-- Password Strength Bar -->
              <div class="d-flex gap-1 mt-2" style="height: 4px;">
                <div class="flex-fill rounded bg-secondary bg-opacity-25" id="s1"></div>
                <div class="flex-fill rounded bg-secondary bg-opacity-25" id="s2"></div>
                <div class="flex-fill rounded bg-secondary bg-opacity-25" id="s3"></div>
                <div class="flex-fill rounded bg-secondary bg-opacity-25" id="s4"></div>
              </div>
              <small class="text-secondary font-mono d-block mt-1" id="strength-lbl" style="font-size: 0.7rem;">Password strength</small>
            </div>

            <!-- Submit Button -->
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-vs-cyan btn-lg w-100 font-mono text-uppercase fw-bold mt-2 py-3">
              Sign In &nbsp;<i class="bi bi-arrow-right"></i>
            </button>
          </form>

          <div class="text-center mt-4 pt-3 border-top border-secondary border-opacity-25 font-mono small text-secondary">
            Don't have an account?
            <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="text-info text-decoration-none fw-bold ms-1">Register Now</a>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div class="text-center mt-3 font-mono small">
          <span class="badge bg-success bg-opacity-10 text-success border border-success border-opacity-25 px-3 py-1.5">
            <i class="bi bi-shield-check me-1"></i>AES-256 &middot; TLS 1.3 &middot; Zero-Knowledge
          </span>
        </div>

    </div>

<script>
  const pwdEl  = document.getElementById('password');
  const bars   = ['s1','s2','s3','s4'].map(id => document.getElementById(id));
  const lbl    = document.getElementById('strength-lbl');
  const colors = ['danger','warning','info','success'];
  const labels = ['Weak','Fair','Good','Strong'];

  function score(p) {
    let s = 0;
    if (p.length >= 8) s++;
    if (p.length >= 12) s++;
    if (/[A-Z]/.test(p) && /[a-z]/.test(p)) s++;
    if (/\d/.test(p) && /[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(p)) s++;
    return Math.min(s, 4);
  }

  pwdEl.addEventListener('input', function () {
    const s = score(this.value);
    bars.forEach((b, i) => {
      b.className = 'flex-fill rounded';
      if (i < s) {
        b.classList.add('bg-' + colors[s - 1]);
      } else {
        b.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'bg-opacity-25');
      }
    });
    lbl.textContent = this.value ? (labels[s - 1] || 'Weak') : 'Password strength';
  });

  document.getElementById('pwd-toggle').addEventListener('click', function () {
    const isText = pwdEl.type === 'text';
    pwdEl.type = isText ? 'password' : 'text';
    document.getElementById('eye-icon').className = isText ? 'bi bi-eye' : 'bi bi-eye-slash';
  });
</script>

{% endblock %}

{% block title %} Create Secure Account | VaultShare {% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<section class="py-5 d-flex align-items-center min-vh-100 position-relative" style="background: radial-gradient(120% 100% at 50% 0%, #0d1e30 0%, var(--vs-bg-dark) 70%);">

  <div class="container">
    <div class="row justify-content-center">

<script>
  /* Toggle Password Visibility */
  function togglePassword(inputId, iconId) {
    const inp = document.getElementById(inputId);
    const ico = document.getElementById(iconId);
    if (inp.type === 'password') { inp.type = 'text'; ico.className = 'bi bi-eye-slash-fill'; }
    else { inp.type = 'password'; ico.className = 'bi bi-eye-fill'; }
  }
  document.getElementById('togglePwd1').addEventListener('click', () => togglePassword('id_password', 'eyeIcon1'));
  document.getElementById('togglePwd2').addEventListener('click', () => togglePassword('id_confirm_password', 'eyeIcon2'));

  /* Password Strength */
  document.getElementById('id_password').addEventListener('input', function () {
    const v = this.value; let s = 0;
    if (v.length >= 8) s++;
    if (/[A-Z]/.test(v)) s++;
    if (/[0-9]/.test(v)) s++;
    if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(v)) s++;
    const cfg = [
      { w:'0%', bg:'transparent', t:'Password strength' },
      { w:'25%', bg:'#ef4444', t:'Weak' },
      { w:'50%', bg:'#f59e0b', t:'Fair' },
      { w:'75%', bg:'#38bdf8', t:'Good' },
      { w:'100%', bg:'#10b981', t:'Strong ✓' },
    ];
    const bar = document.getElementById('strengthFill');
    const label = document.getElementById('strengthText');
    bar.style.width = cfg[s].w;
    bar.style.background = cfg[s].bg;
    label.textContent = cfg[s].t;
    label.style.color = s > 0 ? cfg[s].bg : '';
  });

  /* Avatar preview */
  document.getElementById('id_profile_picture').addEventListener('change', function () {
    const preview = document.getElementById('avatarPreview');
    if (this.files && this.files[0]) {
      const r = new FileReader();
      r.onload = e => {
        preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${e.target.result}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%;" alt="Preview">`;
      };
      r.readAsDataURL(this.files[0]);
    }
  });

  /* Client Validation */
  document.getElementById('registerForm').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    let ok = true;
    const show = (id, cond) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      cond ? (el.classList.remove('d-none'), ok = false) : el.classList.add('d-none');
    };
    show('err-full-name', document.getElementById('id_full_name').value.trim().length < 2);
    show('err-username', document.getElementById('id_username').value.trim().length < 3);
    show('err-email', !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(document.getElementById('id_email').value.trim()));
    show('err-password', document.getElementById('id_password').value.length < 8);
    show('err-confirm', document.getElementById('id_password').value !== document.getElementById('id_confirm_password').value);
    if (!ok) e.preventDefault();
  });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    path('login.html',signin,name='login'),

        "security/",
        include("security_ai.urls")
    ),

    urlpatterns += static(
        settings.MEDIA_URL,
        document_root=settings.MEDIA_ROOT
    )

======================================================================
FILE: accounts\utils.py

def format_size(size):
    if size < 1024:
        return f"{size} B"
    elif size < 1024**2:
        return f"{size/1024:.2f} KB"
    elif size < 1024**3:
        return f"{size/(1024**2):.2f} MB"
    else:
        return f"{size/(1024**3):.2f} GB"

import os
import random
import tempfile
from datetime import timedelta

from accounts.models import SecureShareLink
from encryption.utils import decrypt_file
from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth.tokens import default_token_generator, PasswordResetTokenGenerator
from django.utils.http import urlsafe_base64_encode, urlsafe_base64_decode

def home(request):
    return render(request, 'home.html')

    return render(request, 'about.html')


def signup(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        full_name = request.POST.get("full_name")
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        phone_number = request.POST.get("phone_number")
        password = "[REDACTED]"
        confirm_password = request.POST.get("confirm_password")

        if password != confirm_password:
            messages.error(request, "Passwords do not match.")
            return redirect("signup")
        if User.objects.filter(email=email).exists():
            messages.error(request, "Email already exists.")
            return redirect("signup")

        user = User.objects.create_user(
            email=email,
            password="[REDACTED]"
            full_name=full_name,
            phone_number=phone_number)
        user.is_active = False
        user.save()
        uid = urlsafe_base64_encode(force_bytes(user.pk))
        token = default_token_generator.make_token(user)

        current_site = get_current_site(request)

        verification_link = (f"http://{current_site.domain}/accounts/activate/{uid}/{token}/")

        send_email_async(
            subject="Verify Your VaultShare Account",
            message=f"Click the link below to verify your email:\n\n{verification_link}",
            recipient=user.email,
        )

        messages.success(request, "Account created successfully. Please Verify Your Account via email. ")

        private_key, public_key = generate_rsa_key_pair()
        save_keys(user.id, private_key, public_key)

        return redirect("login")

    return render(request, "signup.html")


def activate(request, uid, token):
    try:
        uid = force_str(urlsafe_base64_decode(uid))
        user = User.objects.get(pk=uid)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError, User.DoesNotExist):
        user = None

    if user is not None and default_token_generator.check_token(user, token):
        user.is_active = True
        user.save()

        create_audit_log(
            user=user,
            action="ACCOUNT_VERIFIED",
            description="Email verified and account activated successfully",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=True,
        )

        messages.success(
            request,
            "Your email has been verified successfully. You can now log in."
        )
        return redirect("login")

    create_audit_log(
        user=None,
        action="ACTIVATION_FAILED",
        description="Invalid or expired account activation token accessed",
        request=request,
        event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
        success=False,
    )
    messages.error(request, "Verification link is invalid or has expired.")
    return redirect("signup")


def signin(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        password = "[REDACTED]"

        user = authenticate(
            request,
            username=email,
            password="[REDACTED]"
        )

        if user is not None:

            # Delete old OTPs
            LoginOTP.objects.filter(user=user).delete()

            # Generate 6-digit OTP
            otp = str(random.randint(100000, 999999))

            # Save OTP
            LoginOTP.objects.create(
                user=user,
                otp=otp,
                expires_at=timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=5)
            )

            # Send OTP email
            send_email_async(
                subject="VaultShare Login Verification",
                message=f"""
Hello {user.first_name},

Your OTP for VaultShare login is:

{otp}

This OTP is valid for 5 minutes.

Do not share this OTP with anyone.

Regards,
VaultShare Team
                """,
                recipient=user.email,
            )

            # Store user ID in session
            request.session["otp_user_id"] = user.id

            messages.success(request, "OTP has been sent to your email.")

            return redirect("verify_otp")

        # Record failed login attempt
        failed_user = User.objects.filter(email=email).first()
        create_audit_log(
            user=failed_user,
            action="LOGIN_FAILED",
            description=f"Failed login attempt for '{email}': Invalid email or password",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=False,
            metadata={"attempted_email": email},
        )
        messages.error(request, "Invalid Email or Password")

    # NOTE: this previously rendered "verify_otp.html", which meant the login
    # page itself served the OTP-entry template. Changed to "login.html" —
    # swap in whatever your actual email/password login template is named.
    return render(request, "login.html")


def logout_view(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="LOGOUT",
            description=f"User {request.user.email} logged out successfully",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=True,
        )
    logout(request)
    return redirect('home')


@login_required
def user_dashboard(request):
    files = File.objects.filter(owner=request.user)
    folders = Folder.objects.filter(owner=request.user)
    total_files = files.count()
    total_folders = folders.count()
    shared_files = SharedFile.objects.filter(shared_by=request.user).count()
    used_storage = files.aggregate(total=Sum("file_size"))["total"] or 0
    total_storage = 5 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
    remaining_storage = total_storage - used_storage
    percent_used = (used_storage / total_storage) * 100
    percent_used = round(percent_used, 2)
    display_percent = max(percent_used, 1) if used_storage > 0 else 0

    context = {
        "folders": folders,
        "files": files,
        "recent_files": files.order_by("-uploaded_at")[:5],
        "total_files": total_files,
        "total_folders": total_folders,
        "shared_files": shared_files,
        "used_storage": format_size(used_storage),
        "remaining_storage": format_size(remaining_storage),
        "total_storage": format_size(total_storage),
        "percent_used": round(percent_used, 2),
        "display_percent": display_percent,
    }
    return render(request, "userDash.html", context)


@login_required
def share_file(request, file_id):
    file = get_object_or_404(File, id=file_id, owner=request.user)
    if request.method == "POST":
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        permission = request.POST.get("permission")
        expires = request.POST.get("expires_at") or None
        shared = SharedFile.objects.create(
            file=file,
            shared_by=request.user,
            receiver_email=email,
            permission=permission,
            expires_at=expires
        )
        link = request.build_absolute_uri(reverse("receiver_download", args=[shared.share_token]))
        send_mail(
            subject="VaulShare File Sharing",
            message=f"""You receive  a secure file. 
                  Hello,

{request.user.email} has shared a file with you.

File Name: {file.file_name}

Permission: {permission}

Download Link:
{link}

Regards,
VaultShare
""",
            from_email=settings.EMAIL_HOST_USER,
            recipient_list=[email],
            fail_silently=False,
        )
        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_SHARED",
            description=f"File shared: {file.file_name}",
            request=request,
            event_type="FILE_ACTIVITY",
            success=True,
            metadata={
                "file_id": file.id,
                "file_name": file.file_name
            }
        )
    return redirect("myfiles")


def share_download(request, token):

    share = get_object_or_404(
        SecureShareLink,
        token=token,
        is_active=True
    )

    if share.expires_at and timezone.now() > share.expires_at:
        return HttpResponse("This share link has expired.")

    file = share.file

    encrypted_path = file.file.path

    temp_dir = os.path.join(
        settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
        "temp_download"
    )

    os.makedirs(
        temp_dir,
        exist_ok=True
    )

    # Use a unique temp filename to avoid collisions between concurrent
    # downloads of files that share the same original name.
    fd, decrypted_path = tempfile.mkstemp(
        dir=temp_dir,
        suffix=f"_{file.file_name}"
    )
    os.close(fd)

    decrypt_file(
        encrypted_path,
        decrypted_path,
        file.encrypted_key
    )

    # -------------------------------
    # SHA-256 INTEGRITY VERIFICATION
    # -------------------------------

    current_hash = generate_sha256(decrypted_path)

    audit_user = (
        request.user
        if request.user.is_authenticated
        else None
    )

    if current_hash != file.sha256_hash:

        create_audit_log(
            user=audit_user,
            action="INTEGRITY_FAILURE",
            description=f"Integrity verification failed for {file.file_name}",
            request=request,
            event_type="FILE_SECURITY",
            success=False,
            reason="Stored SHA-256 hash does not match current file hash",
            metadata={
                "file_id": file.id,
                "file_name": file.file_name,
                "expected_hash": file.sha256_hash,
                "current_hash": current_hash,
            }
        )

        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        return HttpResponse(
            "File integrity verification failed. Download blocked.",
            status=403
        )

    # -------------------------------
    # HASH MATCHED
    # -------------------------------

    create_audit_log(
        user=audit_user,
        action="FILE_DOWNLOADED",
        description=f"Downloaded file: {file.file_name}",
        request=request,
        event_type="FILE_ACTIVITY",
        success=True,
        metadata={
            "file_id": file.id,
            "file_name": file.file_name,
            "integrity_verified": True,
        }
    )

    response = FileResponse(
        open(decrypted_path, "rb"),
        as_attachment=True,
        filename=file.file_name
    )
    # Clean up the plaintext temp file from disk once the response has
    # finished streaming, instead of leaving it there indefinitely.
    response._resource_closers.append(lambda: os.remove(decrypted_path) if os.path.exists(decrypted_path) else None)

    return response


def verify_otp(request):

    user_id = request.session.get("otp_user_id")

    if not user_id:
        messages.error(request, "Session expired. Please login again.")
        return redirect("login")

    try:
        user = User.objects.get(id=user_id)
    except User.DoesNotExist:
        request.session.pop("otp_user_id", None)
        messages.error(
            request,
            "User account not found. Please login again."
        )
        return redirect("login")

    if request.method == "POST":

        entered_otp = request.POST.get("otp")

        try:
            otp_record = LoginOTP.objects.filter(
                user=user,
                is_verified=False
            ).latest("created_at")

        except LoginOTP.DoesNotExist:
            messages.error(request, "OTP not found.")
            return redirect("login")

        if otp_record.is_expired():
            create_audit_log(
                user=user,
                action="OTP_FAILED",
                description=f"OTP verification failed for {user.email}: Passcode expired",
                request=request,
                event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
                success=False,
            )
            messages.error(request, "OTP has expired.")
            otp_record.delete()
            return redirect("login")

        if otp_record.otp != entered_otp:
            create_audit_log(
                user=user,
                action="OTP_FAILED",
                description=f"OTP verification failed for {user.email}: Invalid passcode submitted",
                request=request,
                event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
                success=False,
                metadata={"entered_length": len(entered_otp) if entered_otp else 0},
            )
            messages.error(request, "Invalid OTP.")
            return render(request, "verify_otp.html")

        otp_record.is_verified = True
        otp_record.save()

        login(request, user)

        request.session.pop("otp_user_id", None)
        create_audit_log(
            user=user,
            action="OTP_VERIFIED",
            description="OTP verified successfully",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=True,
        )
        create_audit_log(
            user=user,
            action="LOGIN_SUCCESS",
            description=f"User {user.email} logged in successfully via 2FA OTP",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=True,
        )
        return redirect("user_dashboard")

    return render(request, "verify_otp.html")


def forgot_password(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        User = get_user_model()
        try:
            user = User.objects.get(email=email)
        except User.DoesNotExist:
            create_audit_log(
                user=None,
                action="PASSWORD_RESET_FAILED",
                description=f"Password reset requested for non-existent account: {email}",
                request=request,
                event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
                success=False,
                metadata={"attempted_email": email},
            )
            messages.error(request, "Account Doesn't Exist.")
            return redirect("forgot_password")
        uid = urlsafe_base64_encode(force_bytes(user.pk))
        token = PasswordResetTokenGenerator().make_token(user)
        reset_link = request.build_absolute_uri(reverse("reset_password", kwargs={"uidb64": uid, "token": token, }))
        send_email_async(
            subject="VaultShare Password Reset",
            message=f"""Hello {user.full_name},

We received a request to reset your VaultShare password. Click the link below:
{reset_link}

If you didn't request this, you can safely ignore this email.

Regards,
VaultShare Team""",
            recipient=user.email,
        )
        create_audit_log(
            user=user,
            action="PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED",
            description=f"Password reset link sent to {user.email}",
            request=request,
            event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
            success=True,
        )
        messages.success(request, "Password Reset link has been sent to your email.", )
        return redirect("login")
    return render(request, "forgot_password.html")

def profile(request):
    return render(request, 'profile.html')


@login_required
def audit_trail(request):

    logs = AuditLog.objects.filter(
        user=request.user
    )

    return render(request, "audit-trail.html", {"logs": logs})


@login_required
def edit_profile(request):

    if request.method == "POST":
        form = ProfileUpdateForm(
            request.POST,
            instance=request.user
        )

        if form.is_valid():
            form.save()

            messages.success(
                request,
                "Your profile has been updated successfully."
            )

            return redirect("profile")

    else:
        form = ProfileUpdateForm(instance=request.user)

    return render(
        request,
        "edit_profile.html",
        {"form": form}
    )


@login_required
def change_password(request):

    if request.method == "POST":

        form = PasswordChangeForm(
            request.user,
            request.POST
        )

        if form.is_valid():

            user = form.save()

            update_session_auth_hash(request, user)
            create_audit_log(user=user, action="PASSWORD_CHANGE", description="User updated account password", request=request)

            messages.success(
                request,
                "Your password has been changed successfully."
            )

            return redirect("profile")

    else:

        form = PasswordChangeForm(request.user)

    return render(
        request,
        "change_password.html",
        {"form": form}
    )

======================================================================
FILE: encryption\aes_utils.py
======================================================================
import os
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM


def generate_aes_key():
    """
    Generate a random 256-bit AES key.
    """
    return AESGCM.generate_key(bit_length=256)


def encrypt_file(input_path, output_path):
    """
    Encrypt a file using AES-256-GCM.

    Returns:
        aes_key (bytes)
        nonce (bytes)
    """

    # Generate AES-256 key
    aes_key = generate_aes_key()

    # AES-GCM requires a 12-byte nonce
    nonce = os.urandom(12)

    # Read original file
    with open(input_path, "rb") as file:
        plaintext = file.read()

    # Create AES cipher
    aesgcm = AESGCM(aes_key)

    # Encrypt
    ciphertext = aesgcm.encrypt(
        nonce,
        plaintext,
        None
    )

    # Save encrypted file
    with open(output_path, "wb") as file:
        file.write(ciphertext)

    return aes_key, nonce


def decrypt_file(input_path, output_path, aes_key, nonce):
    """
    Decrypt an AES-256-GCM encrypted file.
    """
    if isinstance(aes_key, memoryview):
        aes_key = bytes(aes_key)
    if isinstance(nonce, memoryview):
        nonce = bytes(nonce)

    with open(input_path, "rb") as file:
        ciphertext = file.read()

    aesgcm = AESGCM(aes_key)

    plaintext = aesgcm.decrypt(
        nonce,
        ciphertext,
        None
    )

    with open(output_path, "wb") as file:
        file.write(plaintext)

======================================================================
FILE: encryption\hash_utils.py
======================================================================
import hashlib


def generate_sha256(file_path):
    sha256 = hashlib.sha256()

    with open(file_path, "rb") as file:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: file.read(4096), b""):
            sha256.update(chunk)

    return sha256.hexdigest()







======================================================================
FILE: encryption\key_manager.py
======================================================================
import os
from django.conf import settings
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization

def save_keys(user_id, private_key, public_key):
    """
    Save RSA keys to media/keys/
    """

    key_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "keys")

    os.makedirs(key_dir, exist_ok=True)

    private_path = os.path.join(key_dir, f"{user_id}_private.pem")
    public_path = os.path.join(key_dir, f"{user_id}_public.pem")

    with open(private_path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(private_key)

    with open(public_path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(public_key)

    return private_path, public_path


def load_private_key(user_id):

    path = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "keys", f"{user_id}_private.pem")

    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        return serialization.load_pem_private_key(
            f.read(),
            password="[REDACTED]"
        )


def load_public_key(user_id):

    path = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "keys", f"{user_id}_public.pem")

    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        return serialization.load_pem_public_key(
            f.read()
        )

======================================================================
FILE: encryption\rsa_utils.py
======================================================================
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import rsa,padding
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization,hashes


def generate_rsa_key_pair():
    """
    Generate a 2048-bit RSA key pair.

    Returns:
        tuple(bytes, bytes): (private_key_pem, public_key_pem)
    """

    private_key = rsa.generate_private_key(
        public_exponent=65537,
        key_size=2048
    )

    public_key = private_key.public_key()

    private_pem = private_key.private_bytes(
        encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
        format=serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8,
        encryption_algorithm=serialization.NoEncryption()
    )

    public_pem = public_key.public_bytes(
        encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
        format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo
    )

    return private_pem, public_pem

def encrypt_aes_key(public_key, aes_key):
    """
    Encrypt the AES key using the recipient's RSA public key.
    """
    if isinstance(aes_key, memoryview):
        aes_key = bytes(aes_key)

    encrypted_key = public_key.encrypt(
        aes_key,
        padding.OAEP(
            mgf=padding.MGF1(algorithm=hashes.SHA256()),
            algorithm=hashes.SHA256(),
            label=None
        )
    )

    return encrypted_key

def decrypt_aes_key(private_key, encrypted_aes_key):
    """
    Decrypt the AES key using the owner's RSA private key.
    """
    if isinstance(encrypted_aes_key, memoryview):
        encrypted_aes_key = bytes(encrypted_aes_key)

    aes_key = private_key.decrypt(
        encrypted_aes_key,
        padding.OAEP(
            mgf=padding.MGF1(algorithm=hashes.SHA256()),
            algorithm=hashes.SHA256(),
            label=None
        )
    )

    return aes_key

FILE: encryption\utils.py

import os
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
import secrets
import string
def generate_key():
    """
    Generate a new encryption key.
    """
    return Fernet.generate_key()


def encrypt_file(input_path, output_path):
    """
    Encrypt a file and save it to output_path.
    """

    key = generate_key()

    cipher = Fernet(key)

    with open(input_path, "rb") as file:
        data = file.read()

    encrypted_data = cipher.encrypt(data)

    with open(output_path, "wb") as file:
        file.write(encrypted_data)

    return key

def decrypt_file(encrypted_path,output_path,key):
    if isinstance(key, str):
        key = key.encode()
   

    cipher=Fernet(key)
    with open(encrypted_path,"rb") as f:
        encrypted_data=f.read()
    decrypted_data=cipher.decrypt(encrypted_data)
    with open(output_path,"wb") as f:
        f.write(decrypted_data)

from django import forms
from accounts.models import File, Folder

class FileUploadForm(forms.ModelForm):

    class Meta:
        model = File
        fields = ['file', 'folder', 'encryption_type']

    def clean_file(self):
        uploaded_file = self.cleaned_data['file']

        # Maximum 50 MB
        if uploaded_file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise forms.ValidationError("Maximum file size is 50 MB.")

        return uploaded_file


class ShareFileForm(forms.Form):

        receiver_email=forms.EmailField(label="Recipient Email")

        permission=forms.ChoiceField(
            choices=[
                ('view','View'),
                ('download','Download')
            ]
        )
        

        expiry_date=forms.DateTimeField(required=False,widget=forms.DateTimeInput(attrs={'type':'datetime-local'}))

            <tr class="text-secondary font-mono small border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25"
              style="font-size: 0.75rem">
              <th scope="col" class="ps-4 py-3">FILE NAME</th>
              <th scope="col" class="py-3">SHARED BY</th>
              <th scope="col" class="py-3">PERMISSION</th>

                ACCESS &amp; DOWNLOAD
              </th>

                </div>

                <span class="badge bg-info bg-opacity-15 text-info border border-info border-opacity-30">View
                  Only</span>

                  class="badge bg-success bg-opacity-15 text-light border border-success border-opacity-30">Download</span>

                <span class="badge bg-secondary bg-opacity-25 text-secondary border border-secondary">Never</span>
                {% endif %}
              </td>

                  class="badge bg-danger bg-opacity-25 text-danger border border-danger font-mono me-2">
                  File Expired
                  </span>

                  <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm font-mono" disabled>

                  </button>

                    Request Access
                  </a>
                  {% endif %}

                  {% elif not share.is_active %}

                  <span class="badge bg-secondary bg-opacity-25 text-secondary border border-secondary font-mono me-2">
                    Revoked
                  </span>

                  {% if share.already_requested %}
                  <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm font-mono" disabled>
                    Request Sent
                  </button>
                  {% else %}
                  <a href="{% url 'request_access_again' share.share_token %}" class="btn btn-warning btn-sm font-mono">
                    Request Access
                  </a>
                  {% endif %}

                  {% elif share.current_downloads >= share.max_downloads %}

                  <span class="badge bg-warning bg-opacity-25 text-warning border border-warning font-mono me-2">
                    Limit Reached
                  </span>

                  {% if share.already_requested %}
                  <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm font-mono" disabled>

                  </button>

                  </a>

                    <i class="bi bi-shield-check me-1"></i>
                    Verify Integrity
                  </a>

                  <button type="button" class="btn btn-vs-cyan btn-sm font-mono my-2" data-bs-toggle="modal"

                  {% endif %}

<script>
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
    const dropZone = document.getElementById('dropZone');
    const dropContent = document.getElementById('dropZoneContent');
    const previewContent = document.getElementById('fileSelectedPreview');
    const nameDisplay = document.getElementById('fileNameDisplay');
    const sizeDisplay = document.getElementById('fileSizeDisplay');

    fileInput.addEventListener('change', function() {
        if (this.files && this.files[0]) {
            const file = this.files[0];
            nameDisplay.textContent = file.name;
            sizeDisplay.textContent = (file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2) + ' MB';
            dropContent.classList.add('d-none');
            previewContent.classList.remove('d-none');
            dropZone.classList.add('border-info');
        }
    });

    ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(eventName => {
        dropZone.addEventListener(eventName, (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropZone.classList.add('dragover');
        }, false);
    });

    ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(eventName => {
        dropZone.addEventListener(eventName, (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
        }, false);
    });
</script>

{% endblock %}

FILE: files\urls.py

from django.urls import path
from . import views

urlpatterns = [

    path("upload/",views.upload_file,name="upload_file"),
    path("my-files/",views.myfiles,name="myfiles",),
    path("delete/<int:file_id>/",views.delete_file,name="delete_file"),
    path("download/<int:file_id>/",views.download_file,name="download_file"),
    path("folders/", views.my_folders, name="my_folders"),
    path("folder/create/",views.create_folder, name = "create_folder"),
    path("folder/<int:folder_id>/",views.folder_details,name="folder_detail"),
    path("folder/delete/<int:folder_id>/",views.delete_folder,name="delete_folder"),
    path("folder/<int:folder_id>/upload/",views.upload_to_folder, name="upload_to_folder"),
    path("share/<int:file_id>/",views.share_file,name="share_file"),

import secrets,string

def generate_random_password(length=12):
    characters = (string.ascii_letters + string.digits +
        "!@&*"
    )

    return ''.join(secrets.choice(characters) for _ in range(length))

@login_required
@login_required
def upload_file(request):

    if request.method == "POST":

        uploaded_file = request.FILES["file"]

        folder = request.POST.get("folder")
        encryption = request.POST.get("encryption_type")
        expiry = request.POST.get("expiry")

        # Validate encryption type before creating encrypted data
        if encryption not in ["AES256", "Dual"]:
            messages.error(request, "Invalid encryption type.")
            return redirect("myfiles")

        temp_dir = os.path.join(
            settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
            "temp"
        )
        os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # Prevent unsafe filenames
        safe_filename = os.path.basename(
            uploaded_file.name
        )

        # Generate unique ID to prevent filename collisions
        unique_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

        # Unique temporary plaintext file
        temp_filename = f"{unique_id}_{safe_filename}"

        file_path = os.path.join(
            temp_dir,
            temp_filename
        )

        encrypted_dir = os.path.join(
            settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
            "encrypted_files"
        )
        os.makedirs(encrypted_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # Unique encrypted .vault file
        encrypted_filename = (
            f"{unique_id}_{safe_filename}.vault"
        )

        encrypted_path = os.path.join(
            encrypted_dir,
            encrypted_filename
        )

        encrypted_file_created = False

        try:

            # Save original file temporarily
            with open(file_path, "wb+") as destination:

                for chunk in uploaded_file.chunks():
                    destination.write(chunk)

            # ClamAV Virus Scan
            is_clean, scan_message = scan_file(
                file_path
            )

            print(
                "ClamAV Result:",
                scan_message
            )

            if not is_clean:

                create_audit_log(
                    user=request.user,
                    action="MALWARE_DETECTED",
                    description=(
                        f"Malware detected during upload: "
                        f"{uploaded_file.name}"
                    ),
                    request=request,
                    event_type="FILE_SECURITY",
                    success=False,
                    reason="ClamAV reported malicious content",
                    metadata={
                        "file_name": uploaded_file.name,
                        "scan_message": scan_message,
                    }
                )

                messages.error(
                    request,
                    f"Upload Rejected: {scan_message}"
                )

                return redirect("myfiles")

            print("ClamAV: File is clean.")

            # Generate SHA-256 of original plaintext
            hash_value = generate_sha256(
                file_path
            )

            print(
                "Generated Hash:",
                hash_value
            )

            print(
                "Length:",
                len(hash_value)
            )

            # Encrypt file using AES-256-GCM
            aes_key, nonce = encrypt_file(
                file_path,
                encrypted_path
            )

            encrypted_file_created = True

            # Store encryption key
            if encryption == "AES256":

                stored_key = aes_key

            elif encryption == "Dual":

                public_key = load_public_key(
                    request.user.id
                )

                stored_key = encrypt_aes_key(
                    public_key,
                    aes_key
                )

            # Relative media path
            relative_path = os.path.relpath(
                encrypted_path,
                settings.MEDIA_ROOT
            ).replace("\\", "/")

            # Save database record
            File.objects.create(
                owner=request.user,
                folder_id=folder if folder else None,
                file_name=safe_filename,
                file=relative_path,
                file_size=uploaded_file.size,
                encryption_type=encryption,
                encrypted_key=stored_key,
                nonce=nonce,
                sha256_hash=hash_value,
            )

            create_audit_log(
                user=request.user,
                action="FILE_UPLOADED",
                description=(
                    f"File uploaded successfully: "
                    f"{safe_filename}"
                ),
                request=request,
                event_type="FILE_ACTIVITY",
                success=True,
                metadata={
                    "file_name": safe_filename,
                    "file_size": uploaded_file.size,
                    "encryption_type": encryption,
                }
            )

            messages.success(
                request,
                "File scanned, encrypted and uploaded successfully."
            )

            return redirect("myfiles")

        except Exception as e:

            print(
                "Upload Error:",
                str(e)
            )

            # Remove encrypted orphan file if it was created
            if encrypted_file_created:
                if os.path.exists(encrypted_path):
                    try:
                        os.remove(encrypted_path)
                    except OSError:
                        pass

            messages.error(
                request,
                "File upload failed. Please try again."
            )

            return redirect("myfiles")

        finally:

            # Always remove plaintext temporary file
            if os.path.exists(file_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(file_path)
                except OSError:
                    pass

    folders = Folder.objects.filter(
        owner=request.user
    )

    return render(
        request,
        "files/upload.html",
        {"folders": folders}
    )

@login_required
def myfiles(request):

    files = File.objects.filter(owner=request.user).order_by("-uploaded_at")

    return render(request, "files/myfiles.html", {"files": files})

@login_required

    file = get_object_or_404(
        File,
        id=file_id,
        owner=request.user
    )

    file.delete()

    return redirect('myfiles')


@login_required
def download_file(request, file_id):
    file = get_object_or_404(
        File,
        id=file_id,
        owner=request.user
    )

    encrypted_path = file.file.path

    if not os.path.exists(encrypted_path):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "The encrypted file could not be found."
        )
        return redirect("myfiles")

    temp_dir = os.path.join(
        settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
        "temp_download"
    )
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)

    decrypted_path = os.path.join(
        temp_dir,
        file.file_name
    )

    try:
        # Recover AES key
        if file.encryption_type == "AES256":
            aes_key = bytes(file.encrypted_key)

        elif file.encryption_type == "Dual":
            private_key = load_private_key(request.user.id)
            aes_key = decrypt_aes_key(
                private_key,
                bytes(file.encrypted_key)
            )

        else:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Unsupported encryption type."
            )
            return redirect("myfiles")

        # Validate encryption metadata
        if len(aes_key) != 32:
            raise ValueError("Invalid AES-256 key length.")

        if not file.nonce:
            raise ValueError("Missing encryption nonce.")

        nonce = bytes(file.nonce)

        if len(nonce) != 12:
            raise ValueError("Invalid AES-GCM nonce length.")

        # AES-GCM decryption
        decrypt_file(
            encrypted_path,
            decrypted_path,
            aes_key,
            nonce
        )

        # SHA-256 verification
        calculated_hash = generate_sha256(
            decrypted_path
        )

        if file.sha256_hash:
            if calculated_hash.lower() != file.sha256_hash.lower():

                create_audit_log(
                    user=request.user,
                    action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
                    description=(
                        f"Integrity verification failed for "
                        f"'{file.file_name}'."
                    ),
                    request=request,
                )

                if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
                    os.remove(decrypted_path)

                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Download blocked. File integrity verification failed."
                )
                return redirect("myfiles")

        # Successful integrity verification
        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_DOWNLOAD",
            description=(
                f"Downloaded '{file.file_name}' "
                f"after successful integrity verification."
            ),
            request=request,
        )

        response = FileResponse(
            open(decrypted_path, "rb"),
            as_attachment=True,
            filename=file.file_name
        )

        return response

    except InvalidTag:
        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
            description=(
                f"AES-GCM authentication failed for "
                f"'{file.file_name}'. Possible corruption or tampering."
            ),
            request=request,
        )

        messages.error(
            request,
            "Download blocked. The file is corrupted or has been modified."
        )
        return redirect("myfiles")

    except Exception:
        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_DOWNLOAD_FAILED",
            description=(
                f"Secure download failed for '{file.file_name}'."
            ),
            request=request,
        )

        messages.error(
            request,
            "Unable to securely decrypt this file."
        )
        return redirect("myfiles")

    return render(request, "files/my_folders.html", {"folders": folders})


@login_required
def create_folder(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        folder_name = request.POST.get("folder_name")
        if folder_name:
            Folder.objects.create(owner=request.user, name=folder_name)

            messages.success(request, "Folder Created Successfully.")
    return redirect("my_folders")


@login_required
def folder_details(request, folder_id):
    folder = get_object_or_404(Folder, id=folder_id, owner=request.user)
    files = File.objects.filter(folder=folder, owner=request.user).order_by(
        "-uploaded_at"
    )
    return render(
        request, "files/folder_detail.html", {"folder": folder, "files": files}
    )
