    folder = get_object_or_404(Folder, id=folder_id, owner=request.user)
    folder.delete()
    messages.success(request, "Folder deleted successfully.")
    return redirect("my_folders")



@login_required
def upload_to_folder(request, folder_id):

    folder = get_object_or_404(
        Folder,
        id=folder_id,
        owner=request.user
    )

    if request.method == "POST":

        uploaded_file = request.FILES.get("file")
        encryption = request.POST.get("encryption_type")

        if not uploaded_file:
            messages.error(request, "Please select a file.")
            return redirect(
                "folder_detail",
                folder_id=folder.id
            )

        # Safe filename
        safe_filename = os.path.basename(
            uploaded_file.name
            
        )
        unique_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

        temp_dir = os.path.join(
            settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
            "temp"
        )
        os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)

        file_path = os.path.join(
        temp_dir,
        f"{unique_id}_{safe_filename}"
)

        try:
            # Save temporary plaintext
            with open(file_path, "wb+") as destination:
                for chunk in uploaded_file.chunks():
                    destination.write(chunk)

            # ClamAV scan
            is_clean, scan_message = scan_file(
                file_path
            )

            print(
                "ClamAV Result:",
                scan_message
            )

            if not is_clean:
                messages.error(
                    request,
                    f"Upload rejected: {scan_message}"
                )
                return redirect(
                    "folder_detail",
                    folder_id=folder.id
                )

            # SHA-256 of original plaintext
            hash_value = generate_sha256(
                file_path
            )

            # Encryption directory
            encrypted_dir = os.path.join(
                settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
                "encrypted_files"
            )
            os.makedirs(
                encrypted_dir,
                exist_ok=True
            )

            encrypted_filename = (
                f"{unique_id}_{safe_filename}.vault"
            )

            encrypted_path = os.path.join(
                encrypted_dir,
                encrypted_filename
            )

            # AES-256-GCM encryption
            aes_key, nonce = encrypt_file(
                file_path,
                encrypted_path
            )

            # Store AES key
            if encryption == "AES256":

                stored_key = aes_key

            elif encryption == "Dual":

                public_key = load_public_key(
                    request.user.id
                )

                stored_key = encrypt_aes_key(
                    public_key,
                    aes_key
                )

            else:

                messages.error(
                    request,
                    "Invalid encryption type."
                )

                return redirect(
                    "folder_detail",
                    folder_id=folder.id
                )

            # Relative media path
            relative_path = os.path.relpath(
                encrypted_path,
                settings.MEDIA_ROOT
            ).replace("\\", "/")

            # Save database record
            File.objects.create(
                owner=request.user,
                folder=folder,
                file_name=safe_filename,
                file=relative_path,
                file_size=uploaded_file.size,
                encryption_type=encryption,
                encrypted_key=stored_key,
                nonce=nonce,
                sha256_hash=hash_value,
            )

            create_audit_log(
                user=request.user,
                action="upload_file",
                description=(
                    f"Uploaded file to folder: "
                    f"{safe_filename}"
                ),
                request=request,
            )

            messages.success(
                request,
                "File scanned, encrypted and uploaded successfully."
            )

        except Exception:
            # Don't expose internal cryptographic/system errors
            messages.error(
                request,
                "File upload failed. Please try again."
            )

        finally:
            # Always remove plaintext temporary file
            if os.path.exists(file_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(file_path)
                except OSError:
                    pass

    return redirect(
        "folder_detail",
        folder_id=folder.id
    )
@login_required
def share_file(request, file_id):
    file = get_object_or_404(File, id=file_id, owner=request.user)

    if request.method == "POST":
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        print(f"Entered Email: '{email}'")
        permission = request.POST.get("permission")
        expires = request.POST.get("expires_at")
        try:
            receiver = User.objects.get(email=email)
        except User.DoesNotExist:
            messages.error(request, "User Not Found.")
            return redirect("myfiles")

        password="[REDACTED]"
        with transaction.atomic():
            
            shared = SharedFile.objects.create(
                file=file,
                shared_by=request.user,
                shared_with=receiver,
                receiver_email=receiver.email,
                permission=permission,
                share_password=make_password(password),
                expires_at=parse_datetime(expires) if expires else None,
            )

            sender = (
            request.user.get_full_name()
            or request.user.first_name
            or request.user.email
            )

            Notification.objects.create(
            user=receiver,
            title="New File Shared",
            message=f"{sender} shared '{file.file_name}' with you.",
            )
            link = request.build_absolute_uri(
            reverse("download_shared_file", args=[shared.share_token])
            )
            send_email_async(
                subject="VaultShare - File Shared",
                message=f"""
Hello {receiver.full_name},

{request.user.full_name} has shared a file with you.

File Name: {file.file_name}
One-Time Password:{password}

Please log in to VaultShare to access it.

Regards,
VaultShare Team
""",
                recipient=receiver.email,
            )

            create_audit_log(user=request.user, action="Shared File",description=f"Shared File: {shared.file.file_name}",request=request)
            messages.success(request, "File Shared Successfully.")

    return redirect("myfiles")

def generate_share_link(request, file_id):
    file = get_object_or_404(File, id=file_id, owner=request.user)
    
    if request.method == "POST":
        password = "[REDACTED]"
        expiry_minutes = int(request.POST.get("expiry", 60))
        expires = timezone.now() + timedelta(minutes=expiry_minutes)

        try:
            share = SecureShareLink.objects.create(
                file=file,
                created_by=request.user,
                password="[REDACTED]"
                expires_at=expires,
            )

            print("Share Created!")
            print("ID:", share.id)
            print("Token:", share.token)

            link = request.build_absolute_uri(
                reverse("download_public_file", args=[share.token])
            )

            print("Generated Link:", link)

            messages.success(request, f"Share Link: {link}")
            create_audit_log(user=request.user, action="generate_share_link",description=f"Share Link Generated: {file.file_name}",request=request)

        except Exception as e:
            print("ERROR:", e)
            messages.error(request, str(e))

    return redirect("myfiles")


def download_public_file(request, token):
    share = get_object_or_404(
        SecureShareLink,
        token=token,
        is_active=True
    )

    if request.method != "POST":
        return render(
            request,
            "files/shared_password.html",
            {"token": token}
        )

    if share.expires_at and timezone.now() > share.expires_at:
        share.is_active = False
        share.save(update_fields=["is_active"])

        messages.error(
            request,
            "This link has expired."
        )
        return redirect("home")

    password = "[REDACTED]"

    if not check_password(password, share.password):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Incorrect password."
        )
        return redirect(
            "download_shared_file",
            token=token
        )

    file = share.file
    encrypted_path = file.file.path

    if not os.path.exists(encrypted_path):
        messages.error(
            request,
            "The encrypted file could not be found."
        )
        return redirect("home")

    temp_dir = os.path.join(
        settings.MEDIA_ROOT,
        "temp_download"
    )
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)

    decrypted_path = os.path.join(
        temp_dir,
        file.file_name
    )

    try:

        if file.encryption_type == "AES256":

            aes_key = bytes(
                file.encrypted_key
            )

        elif file.encryption_type == "Dual":

            private_key = load_private_key(
                file.owner.id
            )

            aes_key = decrypt_aes_key(
                private_key,
                bytes(file.encrypted_key)
            )

        else:
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Unsupported encryption type."
            )
            return redirect("home")

        nonce = bytes(file.nonce)

        if len(aes_key) != 32 or len(nonce) != 12:
            raise ValueError(
                "Invalid encryption metadata."
            )

        # AES-GCM authentication
        decrypt_file(
            encrypted_path,
            decrypted_path,
            aes_key,
            nonce
        )

        # SHA-256 integrity verification
        calculated_hash = generate_sha256(
            decrypted_path
        )

        if (
            file.sha256_hash
            and calculated_hash.lower()
            != file.sha256_hash.lower()
        ):

            create_audit_log(
                user=(
                    request.user
                    if request.user.is_authenticated
                    else None
                ),
                action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
                description=(
                    f"Shared file integrity verification "
                    f"failed for '{file.file_name}'."
                ),
                request=request,
            )

            messages.error(
                request,
                "Download blocked. File integrity verification failed."
            )

            return redirect("home")

        return FileResponse(
            open(decrypted_path, "rb"),
            as_attachment=True,
            filename=file.file_name
        )

    except InvalidTag:

        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        messages.error(
            request,
            "Download blocked. The file is corrupted or has been modified."
        )

        return redirect("home")

    except Exception:

        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        messages.error(
            request,
            "Unable to securely decrypt this file."
        )

        return redirect("home")

def download_shared_file(request, token):
    share = get_object_or_404(SharedFile, share_token=token)
    
    if not share.is_active:
        messages.error(request, "This share link has been revoked.")
        return redirect("received_files" if request.user.is_authenticated else "home")

    if share.expires_at and timezone.now() >= share.expires_at:
        share.is_active = False
        share.save()
        messages.error(request, "This share link has expired.")
        return redirect("received_files" if request.user.is_authenticated else "home")

    if share.current_downloads >= share.max_downloads:
        messages.error(request, "Download limit reached for this shared file.")
        return redirect("received_files" if request.user.is_authenticated else "home")

    if not request.user.is_authenticated:
        messages.error(request, "Please login first.")
        return redirect("login")

    if share.shared_with != request.user:
        messages.error(request, "You are not authorized to access this file.")
        return redirect("received_files")

    if request.method == "GET":
        return render(
            request,
            "files/shared_password.html",
            {"token": token, "type": "shared"},
        )

    password = "[REDACTED]"
    if not check_password(password, share.share_password):
        messages.error(request, "Incorrect password.")
        return redirect("download_shared_file", token=token)

    file = share.file
    encrypted_path = file.file.path
    temp_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "temp_download")
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)
    decrypted_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, file.file_name)

    if file.encryption_type == "AES256":
        aes_key = bytes(file.encrypted_key)
    elif file.encryption_type == "Dual":
        private_key = load_private_key(file.owner.id)
        aes_key = decrypt_aes_key(private_key, bytes(file.encrypted_key))
    else:
        messages.error(request, "Unsupported encryption type.")
        return redirect("received_files")

    try:
        decrypt_file(
            encrypted_path,
            decrypted_path,
            aes_key,
            bytes(file.nonce) if file.nonce else None,
        )

        current_hash = generate_sha256(decrypted_path)

        if file.sha256_hash and current_hash.lower() != file.sha256_hash.lower():
            if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
                os.remove(decrypted_path)

            create_audit_log(
                user=request.user,
                action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
                description=(
                    f"Shared file SHA-256 verification failed "
                    f"for '{file.file_name}'."
                ),
                request=request,
            )
            messages.error(
                request,
                "Integrity verification failed! The file may have been tampered with.",
            )
            return redirect("received_files")

        share.current_downloads += 1
        share.save(update_fields=["current_downloads"])

        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_DOWNLOAD",
            description=(
                f"Shared file '{file.file_name}' downloaded successfully "
                f"after integrity verification."
            ),
            request=request,
        )

        return FileResponse(
            open(decrypted_path, "rb"),
            as_attachment=True,
            filename=file.file_name,
        )

    except InvalidTag:
        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
            description=(
                f"AES-GCM authentication failed for shared file "
                f"'{file.file_name}'. Possible corruption or tampering."
            ),
            request=request,
        )
        messages.error(
            request,
            "Download blocked. The file is corrupted or has been modified.",
        )
        return redirect("received_files")

    except Exception:
        if os.path.exists(decrypted_path):
            os.remove(decrypted_path)

        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_DOWNLOAD_FAILED",
            description=(
                f"Secure shared download failed for '{file.file_name}'."
            ),
            request=request,
        )
        messages.error(request, "Download failed. Please try again.")
        return redirect("received_files")

def received_file(request):
    received = list(
        SharedFile.objects.filter(
            shared_with=request.user
        ).select_related(
            "file",
            "shared_by"
        )
    )

    requested_share_ids = set(
        AccessRequest.objects.filter(
            shared_file__in=received,
            requested_by=request.user
        ).values_list(
            "shared_file_id",
            flat=True
        )
    )

    for share in received:
        share.already_requested = share.id in requested_share_ids

    return render(
        request,
        "files/received_files.html",
        {
            "received": received,
            "now": timezone.now(),
        },
    )

def notification(request):
    notification = Notification.objects.filter(user=request.user).order_by(
        "-created_at"
    )

    return render(request, "files/notification.html", {"notification": notification})


def shared_history(request):
    shared_files = (
        SharedFile.objects.filter(shared_by=request.user)
        .select_related("file", "shared_with")
        .order_by("-shared_at")
    )
    now = timezone.now()
    for share in shared_files:
        if not share.is_active:
            share.status = "Revoked"

        elif share.expires_at and share.expires_at <= now:
            share.status = "Expired"

        elif share.current_downloads >= share.max_downloads:
            share.status = "Download Limit Reached"

        else:
            share.status = "Active"

    return render(
        request,
        "files/shared_history.html",
        {
            "shared_files": shared_files,
            "now": timezone.now(),
        },
    )


def delete_all_notification(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        Notification.objects.filter(user=request.user).delete()
        messages.success(request, "All notifications are deleted.")
    return redirect("notification")


def get_share_status(share):
    if not share.is_active:
        return "Revoked"
    if share.expires_at and timezone.now() >= share.expires_at:
        return "Expired"

    if share.current_downloads >= share.max_downloads:
        return "Download Limit Reached"

    return "Active"


def revoke_share(request, share_id):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return redirect("shared_history")

    share = get_object_or_404(SharedFile, id=share_id, shared_by=request.user)
    if share.current_downloads > 0:
        messages.warning(
            request, "This file has already been downloaded and cannot be revoked."
        )
        return redirect("shared_history")
    if share.expires_at and timezone.now() >= share.expires_at:
        messages.warning(request, "This share has already expired.")
        return redirect("shared_history")

    if not share.is_active:
        messages.info(request, "This share has already been revoked.")
        return redirect("shared_history")
    share.is_active = False
    share.save()

    messages.success(
        request, f"Access to '{share.file.file_name}' has been revoked successfully."
    )
    create_audit_log(
    user=request.user,
    action="SHARE_LINK_REVOKED",
    description=f"Revoked secure share link for: {share.file.file_name}",
    request=request
)
    return redirect("shared_history")


def request_access_again(request, token):
    shared = get_object_or_404(SharedFile, share_token=token)

    if request.user == shared.shared_by:
        messages.error(request, "You cannot request your own file.")
        return redirect("user_dashboard")
    already_requested = AccessRequest.objects.filter(
        shared_file=shared, requested_by=request.user
    ).exists()

    if already_requested:
        messages.warning(request, "You have already requested access for this file.")
        return redirect("received_files")

    AccessRequest.objects.create(shared_file=shared, requested_by=request.user)

    Notification.objects.create(
        user=shared.shared_by,
        title="Access Request",
       message=f"{request.user.full_name or request.user.email} requested access again for '{shared.file.file_name}'."
    )

    messages.success(request, "Your access request has been sent to the sender.")
    return redirect("received_files")
def access_requests(request):

    requests = AccessRequest.objects.filter(
        shared_file__shared_by=request.user,
        status="Pending"
    ).select_related(
        "requested_by",
        "shared_file",
        "shared_file__file",
    )

    return render(
        request,
        "files/access_requests.html",
        {"requests": requests},
    )


@login_required
def approve_request(request, request_id):

    access = get_object_or_404(
        AccessRequest,
        id=request_id,
        status="Pending"
    )

    if access.shared_file.shared_by != request.user:
        messages.error(request, "Unauthorized")
        return redirect("access_requests")

    shared = access.shared_file

    shared.is_active = True
    shared.current_downloads = 0
    shared.max_downloads = 1
    shared.expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(days=2)

    # Generate a new secure link
    shared.share_token = uuid.uuid4()
    new_password=generate_random_password()
    shared.share_password=make_password(new_password)
    shared.save()
    link=request.build_absolute_uri(reverse("download_shared_file", args=[shared.share_token]))
    access.status = "Approved"
    access.save()

    Notification.objects.create(
        user=access.requested_by,
        title="Access Approved",
        message=f"Your request for '{shared.file.file_name}' has been approved. You can download the file once."
    )
    send_email_async(
        subject="VaultShare - Access Approved",
        message=f"""
Hello {access.requested_by.full_name},

Your request to access the file has been approved.

File Name:
{shared.file.file_name}

New Download Link:
{link}

New One-Time Password:
{new_password}

This password replaces the previous one.

You can download this file only once before the link expires.""",
        recipient=access.requested_by.email,
    )
    
    messages.success(request, "Access approved.")

    return redirect("access_requests")


@login_required
def reject_request(request, request_id):

    access = get_object_or_404(
        AccessRequest,
        id=request_id,
        status="Pending"
    )

    if access.shared_file.shared_by != request.user:
        messages.error(request, "Unauthorized")
        return redirect("access_requests")

    access.status = "Rejected"
    access.save()

    Notification.objects.create(
        user=access.requested_by,
        title="Access Rejected",
        message=f"Your request for '{access.shared_file.file.file_name}' has been rejected."
    )

    messages.success(request, "Request rejected.")

    return redirect("access_requests")


@login_required
def verify_file_integrity(request, file_id):
    """
    Cryptographic SHA-256 integrity verification endpoint.
    Decrypts the file payload temporarily in memory to verify SHA-256 checksum authenticity.
    """
    file = None
    try:
        file = File.objects.get(id=file_id, owner=request.user)
    except File.DoesNotExist:
        shared = SharedFile.objects.filter(file_id=file_id, shared_with=request.user).first()
        if shared:
            file = shared.file

    if not file:
        messages.error(request, "File not found or access denied.")
        return redirect("myfiles")

    encrypted_path = file.file.path
    if not os.path.exists(encrypted_path):
        messages.error(request, f"File payload for '{file.file_name}' does not exist on disk.")
        return redirect(request.META.get("HTTP_REFERER", "myfiles"))

    temp_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, "temp_integrity")
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)
    temp_decrypted = os.path.join(temp_dir, f"verify_{uuid.uuid4().hex}_{file.file_name}")

    try:
        if file.encryption_type == "AES256":
            aes_key = bytes(file.encrypted_key)
        else:
            private_key = load_private_key(file.owner.id)
            aes_key = decrypt_aes_key(private_key, bytes(file.encrypted_key))

        decrypt_file(encrypted_path, temp_decrypted, aes_key, bytes(file.nonce) if file.nonce else None)
        calculated_hash = generate_sha256(temp_decrypted)
    except Exception as e:
        messages.error(request, f"Decryption failed during integrity check: {str(e)}")
        return redirect(request.META.get("HTTP_REFERER", "myfiles"))
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp_decrypted):
            try:
                os.remove(temp_decrypted)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if not file.sha256_hash:
        file.sha256_hash = calculated_hash
        file.save(update_fields=["sha256_hash"])

    is_valid = (calculated_hash.lower() == file.sha256_hash.lower())

    if is_valid:
        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_INTEGRITY_VERIFIED",
            description=f"Verified Integrity for '{file.file_name}': SHA-256 Match ({calculated_hash[:16]}...)",
            request=request,
        )
        if request.headers.get("x-requested-with") == "XMLHttpRequest" or request.GET.get("format") == "json":
            return JsonResponse({
                "status": "success",
                "match": True,
                "file_name": file.file_name,
                "expected_hash": file.sha256_hash,
                "calculated_hash": calculated_hash,
                "message": f"Cryptographic Integrity Verified! SHA-256 checksum matched for '{file.file_name}'."
            })
        messages.success(
            request,
            f"✓ SHA-256 Integrity Verified for '{file.file_name}': Checksum ({calculated_hash[:16]}...) matches - File is authentic & untampered."
        )
    else:
        create_audit_log(
            user=request.user,
            action="FILE_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
            description=f"CRITICAL: Integrity Check Failed for '{file.file_name}' (Hash Mismatch!)",
            request=request,
        )
        if request.headers.get("x-requested-with") == "XMLHttpRequest" or request.GET.get("format") == "json":
            return JsonResponse({
                "status": "error",
                "match": False,
                "file_name": file.file_name,
                "expected_hash": file.sha256_hash,
                "calculated_hash": calculated_hash,
                "message": f"CRITICAL INTEGRITY FAILURE! SHA-256 hash mismatch detected for '{file.file_name}'."
            }, status=400)
        messages.error(
            request,
            f"⚠ CRITICAL INTEGRITY WARNING: SHA-256 checksum mismatch for '{file.file_name}'. The file may have been modified or corrupted!"
        )

    return redirect(request.META.get("HTTP_REFERER", "myfiles"))

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'encryptedFileSharing.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: security_ai\baseline.py

import math
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from accounts.models import AuditLog

# Minimum history required to compute a per-user personal baseline
MIN_BASELINE_EVENTS = 10
MIN_BASELINE_SPAN_DAYS = 2

# Global fallback defaults
GLOBAL_BASELINE = {
    "is_custom": False,
    "sample_count": 0,
    "avg_downloads_per_hour": 3.0,
    "std_downloads_per_hour": 2.0,
    "avg_events_per_hour": 6.0,
    "std_events_per_hour": 4.0,
    "max_normal_downloads_burst": 10.0,
}


def get_user_baseline(user):
    """
    Computes a personalized behavioral baseline for a user based on historical audit events.
    If the user has insufficient activity, falls back safely to the global baseline.
    """
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return GLOBAL_BASELINE.copy()

    now = timezone.now()
    cutoff_history = now - timedelta(days=30)
    # Exclude the immediate last 1 hour to prevent an ongoing attack from distorting the baseline
    cutoff_recent = now - timedelta(hours=1)

    historical_logs = list(
        AuditLog.objects.filter(
            user=user,
            created_at__gte=cutoff_history,
            created_at__lte=cutoff_recent,
        ).order_by("created_at")
    )

    total_events = len(historical_logs)
    if total_events < MIN_BASELINE_EVENTS:
        return GLOBAL_BASELINE.copy()

    span_days = (historical_logs[-1].created_at - historical_logs[0].created_at).total_seconds() / 86400.0
    if span_days < MIN_BASELINE_SPAN_DAYS:
        return GLOBAL_BASELINE.copy()

    # Bucket downloads and events into hour slots
    hour_buckets_events = {}
    hour_buckets_downloads = {}

    for log in historical_logs:
        # Group by (YYYY-MM-DD, HH)
        bucket_key = (
            log.created_at.year,
            log.created_at.month,
            log.created_at.day,
            log.created_at.hour,
        )
        hour_buckets_events[bucket_key] = hour_buckets_events.get(bucket_key, 0) + 1
        if "DOWNLOAD" in (log.action or "").upper():
            hour_buckets_downloads[bucket_key] = hour_buckets_downloads.get(bucket_key, 0) + 1

    active_hours = max(1, len(hour_buckets_events))

    # Event rates
    event_counts = list(hour_buckets_events.values())
    avg_events = sum(event_counts) / float(active_hours)
    var_events = sum((x - avg_events) ** 2 for x in event_counts) / float(active_hours)
    std_events = math.sqrt(var_events)

    # Download rates
    dl_counts = list(hour_buckets_downloads.values()) or [0]
    avg_dl = sum(dl_counts) / float(active_hours)
    var_dl = sum((x - avg_dl) ** 2 for x in dl_counts) / float(active_hours)
    std_dl = math.sqrt(var_dl)

    # Upper normal bound for burst downloads (mean + 3*std, minimum 8)
    max_normal_downloads = max(8.0, avg_dl + (3.0 * std_dl))

    return {
        "is_custom": True,
        "sample_count": total_events,
        "avg_downloads_per_hour": round(avg_dl, 2),
        "std_downloads_per_hour": round(std_dl, 2),
        "avg_events_per_hour": round(avg_events, 2),
        "std_events_per_hour": round(std_events, 2),
        "max_normal_downloads_burst": round(max_normal_downloads, 2),
    }


def is_download_rate_abnormal(user, current_downloads_1h):
    """
    Checks whether current 1-hour download volume exceeds the user's normal baseline.
    Returns: (is_abnormal: bool, reason: str, baseline_info: dict)
    """
    baseline = get_user_baseline(user)
    threshold = baseline["max_normal_downloads_burst"]

    if current_downloads_1h > threshold:
        if baseline["is_custom"]:
            diff_percent = int(((current_downloads_1h - baseline["avg_downloads_per_hour"]) / max(1.0, baseline["avg_downloads_per_hour"])) * 100)
            reason = (
                f"Download volume ({int(current_downloads_1h)} files/hr) is significantly above "
                f"user's historical baseline (avg {baseline['avg_downloads_per_hour']}/hr, +{diff_percent}% deviation)"
            )
        else:
            reason = (
                f"Download burst ({int(current_downloads_1h)} files/hr) exceeds safe system baseline "
                f"threshold ({int(threshold)} files/hr)"
            )
        return True, reason, baseline

    return False, "", baseline

from django.conf import settings
import os

# Base directory for persisted models
MODEL_DIR = getattr(
    settings,
    "SECURITY_AI_MODEL_DIR",
    os.path.join(settings.BASE_DIR, "security_ai", "saved_models"),
)

# Isolation Forest Hyperparameters
ISOLATION_FOREST_N_ESTIMATORS = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_N_ESTIMATORS", 100)
ISOLATION_FOREST_CONTAMINATION = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_CONTAMINATION", 0.05)
ISOLATION_FOREST_RANDOM_STATE = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_RANDOM_STATE", 42)
MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TRAINING = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_MIN_TRAIN_SAMPLES", 10)

# Risk Level Cutoffs (0 to 100)
RISK_LEVEL_LOW_MAX = 29
RISK_LEVEL_MEDIUM_MAX = 59
RISK_LEVEL_HIGH_MAX = 79
# >= 80 is CRITICAL

# Threshold for marking AuditLog suspicious and creating/correlating SecurityIncident
SUSPICIOUS_RISK_THRESHOLD = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_SUSPICIOUS_THRESHOLD", 60)

# Incident Deduplication / Correlation Window (in minutes)
INCIDENT_DEDUPLICATION_MINUTES = getattr(settings, "SECURITY_AI_DEDUP_MINUTES", 30)

# Rule-Based Thresholds
RULE_THRESHOLDS = {
    # Authentication
    "BRUTE_FORCE_FAILED_LOGINS": 5,      # Failed logins in 10m
    "BRUTE_FORCE_WINDOW_MINUTES": 10,
    "FAILED_OTP_THRESHOLD": 3,           # Failed OTP attempts in 10m
    "FAILED_OTP_WINDOW_MINUTES": 10,

    # Downloads
    "MASS_DOWNLOAD_BURST_COUNT": 8,      # Downloads in 10m
    "MASS_DOWNLOAD_BURST_WINDOW_MIN": 10,
    "MASS_DOWNLOAD_BASELINE_MULTIPLIER": 3.0,  # 3x standard hourly download baseline

    # Integrity & Malware
    "INTEGRITY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD": 1,    # SHA-256 or AES-GCM tag mismatch is immediately HIGH/CRITICAL
    "MALWARE_DETECTION_THRESHOLD": 1,    # ClamAV detection is immediately CRITICAL

    # Sharing
    "REVOKED_ACCESS_ATTEMPTS": 2,        # Attempts to access revoked file
    "EXPIRED_ACCESS_ATTEMPTS": 3,        # Attempts to access expired file
    "WRONG_SHARE_PASSWORD_ATTEMPTS": 3,  # Incorrect share password attempts
    "BURST_SHARING_COUNT": 10,           # Files shared in 1 hour

    # Network / Origin
    "DISTINCT_IPS_1H_THRESHOLD": 3,      # Number of distinct IPs for same user in 1 hour
}

# Score Weights for Combined Risk Score
# Final Score = (ML_WEIGHT * ml_score) + (RULE_WEIGHT * rule_score) + base_event_penalty
ML_SCORE_WEIGHT = 0.35
RULE_SCORE_WEIGHT = 0.65


======================================================================
FILE: security_ai\engine.py
======================================================================
import logging
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from django.db import transaction

from .config import (
    RISK_LEVEL_LOW_MAX,
    RISK_LEVEL_MEDIUM_MAX,
    RISK_LEVEL_HIGH_MAX,
    SUSPICIOUS_RISK_THRESHOLD,
    INCIDENT_DEDUPLICATION_MINUTES,
    ML_SCORE_WEIGHT,
    RULE_SCORE_WEIGHT,
)
from .features import extract_features, features_to_vector
from .model import model_manager
from .rules import evaluate_rules
from .models import SecurityIncident

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def score_to_risk_level(score):
    if score <= RISK_LEVEL_LOW_MAX:
        return "LOW"
    elif score <= RISK_LEVEL_MEDIUM_MAX:
        return "MEDIUM"
    elif score <= RISK_LEVEL_HIGH_MAX:
        return "HIGH"
    return "CRITICAL"


def evaluate_security_event(audit_log, request=None):
    """
    Core real-time security detection pipeline.
    Executes against the given AuditLog instance after it is saved.
    
    Returns:
        dict: Detection results containing anomaly_score, rule_score, risk_score,
              risk_level, suspicious, reasons, and incident (if created).
    """
    try:
        user = audit_log.user
        ip = audit_log.ip_address

        # 1. Feature Extraction
        features = extract_features(
            user=user,
            ip_address=ip,
            as_of_time=audit_log.created_at or timezone.now(),
            exclude_log_id=audit_log.id,
        )
        feature_vector = features_to_vector(features)

        # 2. Isolation Forest Evaluation
        ml_result = model_manager.predict_anomaly(feature_vector)
        anomaly_score = ml_result.get("anomaly_score", 0.0)  # 0.0 to 1.0
        ml_score_100 = anomaly_score * 100.0

        # 3. Deterministic Security Rules
        rule_result = evaluate_rules(audit_log, features, user=user)
        rule_score = rule_result.get("rule_score", 0.0)
        reasons = list(rule_result.get("reasons", []))

        # Explainability for ML anomaly
        if ml_result.get("is_outlier") and anomaly_score >= 0.65:
            ml_reason = (
                f"Isolation Forest identified behavioral anomaly (score: {anomaly_score:.2f}) "
                f"differing significantly from normal activity patterns."
            )
            reasons.append(ml_reason)

        # 4. Combined Risk Score Calculation
        if rule_score >= 90.0:
            # Critical rules take precedence
            final_risk_score = int(rule_score)
        elif rule_score > 0 and ml_score_100 > 0:
            # Weighted combination
            final_risk_score = int(
                (RULE_SCORE_WEIGHT * rule_score) + (ML_SCORE_WEIGHT * ml_score_100)
            )
        elif rule_score > 0:
            final_risk_score = int(rule_score)
        elif ml_result.get("is_outlier"):
            final_risk_score = int(ml_score_100 * 0.75)
        else:
            final_risk_score = int(ml_score_100 * 0.4)

        final_risk_score = max(0, min(100, final_risk_score))
        risk_level = score_to_risk_level(final_risk_score)
        is_suspicious = (final_risk_score >= SUSPICIOUS_RISK_THRESHOLD)

        # 5. Update AuditLog record
        audit_log.risk_score = final_risk_score
        audit_log.risk_level = risk_level
        audit_log.suspicious = is_suspicious
        if reasons:
            audit_log.reason = "; ".join(reasons)

        current_meta = audit_log.metadata if isinstance(audit_log.metadata, dict) else {}
        current_meta["detection"] = {
            "risk_score": final_risk_score,
            "risk_level": risk_level,
            "anomaly_score": round(anomaly_score, 4),
            "rule_score": round(rule_score, 2),
            "is_outlier": ml_result.get("is_outlier", False),
            "reasons": reasons,
            "model_version": ml_result.get("version", "none"),
        }
        audit_log.metadata = current_meta

        audit_log.save(update_fields=["risk_score", "risk_level", "suspicious", "reason", "metadata"])

        # 6. Incident Creation & Deduplication
        incident = None
        if is_suspicious:
            incident = create_or_update_incident(
                audit_log=audit_log,
                incident_type=(
            rule_result.get("incident_type")
            or "ISOLATION_FOREST_ANOMALY"),
                 severity=risk_level,
                risk_score=final_risk_score,
                anomaly_score=anomaly_score,
                rule_score=rule_score,
                reasons=reasons,
                features=features,
            )

        return {
            "anomaly_score": round(anomaly_score, 4),
            "rule_score": round(rule_score, 2),
            "risk_score": final_risk_score,
            "risk_level": risk_level,
            "suspicious": is_suspicious,
            "reasons": reasons,
            "incident": incident,
        }

    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("Error executing real-time security detection pipeline: %s", e)
        return {
            "anomaly_score": 0.0,
            "rule_score": 0.0,
            "risk_score": audit_log.risk_score or 0,
            "risk_level": audit_log.risk_level or "LOW",
            "suspicious": False,
            "reasons": [f"Detection evaluation error: {str(e)}"],
            "incident": None,
        }


def create_or_update_incident(
    audit_log,
    incident_type,
    severity,
    risk_score,
    anomaly_score,
    rule_score,
    reasons,
    features,
):
    """
    Creates a new SecurityIncident or correlates with an existing open incident
    for the same user/IP and incident type within the deduplication window.
    """
    user = audit_log.user
    ip = audit_log.ip_address
    window_start = timezone.now() - timedelta(minutes=INCIDENT_DEDUPLICATION_MINUTES)

    # Search for an existing open or investigating incident
    existing_qs = SecurityIncident.objects.filter(
        incident_type=incident_type,
        status__in=["OPEN", "INVESTIGATING"],
        created_at__gte=window_start,
    )

    if user:
        existing = existing_qs.filter(user=user).first()
    elif ip:
        existing = existing_qs.filter(ip_address=ip).first()
    else:
        existing = None

    description_text = (
        f"Suspicious activity detected: {audit_log.description}. "
        f"Reasons: {'; '.join(reasons)}"
    )

    evidence_payload = {
        "reasons": reasons,
        "features_snapshot": features,
        "latest_event_id": audit_log.id,
        "latest_action": audit_log.action,
        "last_observed": timezone.now().isoformat(),
    }

    with transaction.atomic():
        if existing:
            # Correlate and escalate if necessary
            existing.risk_score = max(existing.risk_score, risk_score)
            if severity == "CRITICAL" or (severity == "HIGH" and existing.severity in ["LOW", "MEDIUM"]):
                existing.severity = severity
            existing.anomaly_score = max(existing.anomaly_score, anomaly_score)
            existing.rule_score = max(existing.rule_score, rule_score)

            # Update evidence
            past_ev = existing.evidence or {}
            past_reasons = past_ev.get("reasons", [])
            merged_reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(past_reasons + reasons))
            past_ev["reasons"] = merged_reasons
            past_ev["correlation_count"] = past_ev.get("correlation_count", 1) + 1
            past_ev["latest_event_id"] = audit_log.id
            existing.evidence = past_ev
            existing.description = description_text
            existing.save()
            return existing
        else:
            # Create fresh incident
            incident = SecurityIncident.objects.create(
                user=user,
                incident_type=incident_type,
                severity=severity,
                risk_score=risk_score,
                anomaly_score=round(anomaly_score, 4),
                rule_score=round(rule_score, 2),
                description=description_text,
                evidence=evidence_payload,
                source_event=audit_log,
                status="OPEN",
                ip_address=ip,
                user_agent=audit_log.user_agent,
                device_information=audit_log.device_information,
            )
            return incident


======================================================================
FILE: security_ai\features.py

from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from accounts.models import AuditLog

FEATURE_NAMES = [
    "events_5m",
    "events_15m",
    "events_1h",
    "failed_logins_1h",
    "failed_otps_1h",
    "successful_logins_1h",
    "downloads_1h",
    "uploads_1h",
    "shares_1h",
    "access_requests_1h",
    "failed_access_1h",
    "integrity_failures_1h",
    "malware_events_1h",
    "distinct_ips_24h",
    "distinct_devices_24h",
    "auth_failure_ratio",
    "time_since_last_event_sec",
    "different_files_accessed_1h",
    "repeated_resource_access_1h",
]


def extract_features(user=None, ip_address=None, as_of_time=None, exclude_log_id=None):
    """
    Extracts deterministic numerical behavioral features from actual AuditLog records.
    Can extract for an authenticated User, or for an IP address if user is None.
    
    Returns:
        dict: feature_name -> float value
    """
    if as_of_time is None:
        as_of_time = timezone.now()

    # Base queryset
    qs = AuditLog.objects.filter(created_at__lte=as_of_time)
    if exclude_log_id:
        qs = qs.exclude(id=exclude_log_id)

    if user and user.is_authenticated:
        qs = qs.filter(user=user)
    elif ip_address:
        qs = qs.filter(ip_address=ip_address)
    else:
        # Empty context
        return {name: 0.0 for name in FEATURE_NAMES}

    # Time boundaries
    t_5m = as_of_time - timedelta(minutes=5)
    t_15m = as_of_time - timedelta(minutes=15)
    t_1h = as_of_time - timedelta(hours=1)
    t_24h = as_of_time - timedelta(hours=24)

    # 24-hour log cache for efficient aggregate calculations in Python
    recent_logs = list(
    qs.filter(created_at__gte=t_24h)
    .order_by("-created_at")
)

    events_5m = 0
    events_15m = 0
    events_1h = 0
    failed_logins_1h = 0
    failed_otps_1h = 0
    successful_logins_1h = 0
    downloads_1h = 0
    uploads_1h = 0
    shares_1h = 0
    access_requests_1h = 0
    failed_access_1h = 0
    integrity_failures_1h = 0
    malware_events_1h = 0

    ips_24h = set()
    devices_24h = set()
    files_accessed_1h = set()
    resource_counts_1h = {}

    last_event_time = None

    for idx, log in enumerate(recent_logs):
        if idx == 0:
            last_event_time = log.created_at

        if log.ip_address:
            ips_24h.add(log.ip_address)
        if log.user_agent:
            devices_24h.add(log.user_agent)

        action_upper = (log.action or "").upper()
        event_type_upper = (log.event_type or "").upper()

        # 1-hour window features
        if log.created_at >= t_1h:
            events_1h += 1

            if log.created_at >= t_15m:
                events_15m += 1

            if log.created_at >= t_5m:
                events_5m += 1

            # Categorize actions
            if "LOGIN_FAILED" in action_upper or (not log.success and "LOGIN" in action_upper):
                failed_logins_1h += 1
            elif "OTP_FAILED" in action_upper or (not log.success and "OTP" in action_upper):
                failed_otps_1h += 1
            elif "LOGIN" in action_upper or "SIGNIN" in action_upper or "OTP_VERIFIED" in action_upper:
                if log.success:
                    successful_logins_1h += 1

            if "DOWNLOAD" in action_upper:
                downloads_1h += 1
            if "UPLOAD" in action_upper:
                uploads_1h += 1
            if "SHARE" in action_upper:
                shares_1h += 1

            if "ACCESS_REQUEST" in action_upper:
                access_requests_1h += 1

            if (not log.success) or "DENIED" in action_upper or "UNAUTHORIZED" in action_upper or "FAILED" in action_upper or "EXPIRED" in action_upper or "REVOKED" in action_upper:
                failed_access_1h += 1

            if "INTEGRITY" in action_upper or "INTEGRITY" in event_type_upper or "TAMPER" in action_upper:
                if not log.success or "FAILED" in action_upper:
                    integrity_failures_1h += 1

            if "MALWARE" in action_upper or "MALWARE" in event_type_upper:
                malware_events_1h += 1

            # Resource tracking from metadata or description
            resource_key = None
            if isinstance(log.metadata, dict):
                resource_key = log.metadata.get("file_id") or log.metadata.get("file_name")
            if not resource_key and log.description:
                resource_key = log.description[:50]
            if resource_key:
                files_accessed_1h.add(resource_key)
                resource_counts_1h[resource_key] = resource_counts_1h.get(resource_key, 0) + 1

    # Authentication failure ratio
    total_auth = failed_logins_1h + failed_otps_1h + successful_logins_1h
    if total_auth > 0:
        auth_failure_ratio = (failed_logins_1h + failed_otps_1h) / float(total_auth)
    else:
        auth_failure_ratio = 0.0

    # Time since last event in seconds
    if last_event_time:
        time_since_last_event_sec = max(0.0, (as_of_time - last_event_time).total_seconds())
    else:
        time_since_last_event_sec = 86400.0  # Max 1 day default

    # Repeated resource access
       # Repeated resource access
    repeated_resource_access = sum(
        count - 1
        for count in resource_counts_1h.values()
        if count > 1
    )

    return {
        "events_5m": float(events_5m),
        "events_15m": float(events_15m),
        "events_1h": float(events_1h),
        "failed_logins_1h": float(failed_logins_1h),
        "failed_otps_1h": float(failed_otps_1h),
        "successful_logins_1h": float(successful_logins_1h),
        "downloads_1h": float(downloads_1h),
        "uploads_1h": float(uploads_1h),
        "shares_1h": float(shares_1h),
        "access_requests_1h": float(access_requests_1h),
        "failed_access_1h": float(failed_access_1h),
        "integrity_failures_1h": float(integrity_failures_1h),
        "malware_events_1h": float(malware_events_1h),
        "distinct_ips_24h": float(max(1, len(ips_24h))),
        "distinct_devices_24h": float(max(1, len(devices_24h))),
        "auth_failure_ratio": float(auth_failure_ratio),
        "time_since_last_event_sec": float(time_since_last_event_sec),
        "different_files_accessed_1h": float(len(files_accessed_1h)),
        "repeated_resource_access_1h": float(repeated_resource_access),
    }


def features_to_vector(feature_dict):
    """
    Converts feature dictionary into ordered float vector matching FEATURE_NAMES.
    """
    return [float(feature_dict.get(name, 0.0)) for name in FEATURE_NAMES]

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand

from accounts.models import AuditLog

from security_ai.features import (
    extract_features,
    features_to_vector,
)

from security_ai.model import model_manager

from security_ai.config import MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TRAINING


class Command(BaseCommand):

    help = "Train the Security AI Isolation Forest model"

    def handle(self, *args, **options):

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.WARNING(
                "Collecting audit logs..."
            )
        )

        logs = (
            AuditLog.objects
            .select_related("user")
            .order_by("created_at")
        )

        training_data = []

        for log in logs:

            # Prefer normal historical behaviour for training.
            if log.suspicious:
                continue

            features = extract_features(
                user=log.user,
                ip_address=log.ip_address,
                as_of_time=log.created_at,
                exclude_log_id=log.id,
            )

            vector = features_to_vector(features)

            training_data.append(vector)

        sample_count = len(training_data)

        self.stdout.write(
            f"Training samples found: {sample_count}"
        )

        if sample_count < MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TRAINING:

            self.stdout.write(
                self.style.ERROR(
                    f"Not enough training samples. "
                    f"Minimum required: "
                    f"{MIN_SAMPLES_FOR_TRAINING}"
                )
            )

            return

        metadata = model_manager.train(
            training_data,
            version="1.0"
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                "Security AI model trained successfully."
            )
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            f"Samples: {metadata['sample_count']}"
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            f"Features: {metadata['feature_count']}"
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            f"Detected training anomalies: "
            f"{metadata['train_anomalies_detected']}"
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            f"Version: {metadata['version']}"
        )

FILE: security_ai\model.py

import os
import json
import numpy as np
import joblib
from datetime import datetime
from sklearn.ensemble import IsolationForest

from .config import (
    MODEL_DIR,
    ISOLATION_FOREST_N_ESTIMATORS,
    ISOLATION_FOREST_CONTAMINATION,
    ISOLATION_FOREST_RANDOM_STATE,
)
from .features import FEATURE_NAMES

MODEL_FILENAME = "isolation_forest.joblib"
METADATA_FILENAME = "model_metadata.json"


class IsolationForestManager:
    _instance = None
    _model = None
    _metadata = None
    _last_load_mtime = 0

    @classmethod
    def get_instance(cls):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = cls()
        return cls._instance

    @property
    def model_path(self):
        return os.path.join(MODEL_DIR, MODEL_FILENAME)

    @property
    def metadata_path(self):
        return os.path.join(MODEL_DIR, METADATA_FILENAME)

    def load_model(self, force_reload=False):
        """
        Loads the persisted Isolation Forest model and its metadata.
        Caches in memory and reloads if file modification time changes.
        """
        if not os.path.exists(self.model_path):
            self._model = None
            self._metadata = None
            return None, None

        mtime = os.path.getmtime(self.model_path)
        if not force_reload and self._model is not None and mtime == self._last_load_mtime:
            return self._model, self._metadata

        try:
            self._model = joblib.load(self.model_path)
            self._last_load_mtime = mtime
            if os.path.exists(self.metadata_path):
                with open(self.metadata_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._metadata = json.load(f)
            else:
                self._metadata = {"version": "1.0", "trained_at": "unknown"}
            return self._model, self._metadata
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[SecurityAI] Error loading Isolation Forest model: {e}")
            self._model = None
            self._metadata = None
            return None, None

    def predict_anomaly(self, feature_vector):
        """
        Computes anomaly score for a given feature vector using Isolation Forest.
        
        Returns:
            dict: {
                "anomaly_score": float (0.0 to 1.0, higher = more anomalous),
                "is_outlier": bool,
                "model_available": bool,
                "version": str
            }
        """
        model, metadata = self.load_model()
        if model is None:
            return {
                "anomaly_score": 0.0,
                "is_outlier": False,
                "model_available": False,
                "version": "none",
            }

        X = np.array([feature_vector], dtype=float)

        try:
            # decision_function: < 0 is anomaly, > 0 is inlier
            dec = float(model.decision_function(X)[0])
            pred = int(model.predict(X)[0])  # -1 = anomaly, +1 = normal

            # Map decision function (-0.5 to +0.5 typical) to calibrated 0.0 - 1.0 anomaly scale
            # dec <= -0.2 -> ~0.8 to 1.0 (very anomalous)
            # dec == 0.0  -> 0.5 (boundary)
            # dec >= +0.2 -> 0.0 to 0.2 (very normal)
            calibrated_score = 0.5 - (dec * 1.5)
            calibrated_score = max(0.0, min(1.0, calibrated_score))

            is_outlier = (pred == -1)

            return {
                "anomaly_score": round(float(calibrated_score), 4),
                "is_outlier": is_outlier,
                "model_available": True,
                "version": metadata.get("version", "1.0") if metadata else "1.0",
                "raw_decision": round(dec, 4),
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[SecurityAI] Error predicting anomaly: {e}")
            return {
                "anomaly_score": 0.0,
                "is_outlier": False,
                "model_available": False,
                "version": "error",
            }

    
def train(self, X_train, contamination=None, version="1.0"):
    """
    Train Isolation Forest after validating the feature matrix.
    Saves the model and its metadata to disk.
    """
    if contamination is None:
        contamination = ISOLATION_FOREST_CONTAMINATION

    if not 0.0 < contamination <= 0.5:
        raise ValueError("contamination must be greater than 0 and at most 0.5.")

    X = np.asarray(X_train, dtype=float)

    if X.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError("Training data must be a 2D feature matrix.")

    if X.shape[0] < 2:
        raise ValueError("At least 2 training samples are required.")

    if X.shape[1] != len(FEATURE_NAMES):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Expected {len(FEATURE_NAMES)} features per sample; "
            f"received {X.shape[1]}."
        )

    if not np.isfinite(X).all():
        raise ValueError("Training data contains NaN or infinite values.")

    os.makedirs(MODEL_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    model = IsolationForest(
        n_estimators=ISOLATION_FOREST_N_ESTIMATORS,
        contamination=contamination,
        random_state=ISOLATION_FOREST_RANDOM_STATE,
    )

    model.fit(X)

    train_preds = model.predict(X)
    anomaly_count = int(np.sum(train_preds == -1))

    metadata = {
        "version": version,
        "trained_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "sample_count": int(X.shape[0]),
        "feature_count": int(X.shape[1]),
        "feature_names": FEATURE_NAMES,
        "contamination": float(contamination),
        "n_estimators": int(ISOLATION_FOREST_N_ESTIMATORS),
        "random_state": int(ISOLATION_FOREST_RANDOM_STATE),
        "train_anomalies_detected": anomaly_count,
    }

    # Persist the model and metadata only after fitting succeeds.
    joblib.dump(model, self.model_path)

    with open(self.metadata_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)

    self.load_model(force_reload=True)
    return metadata


model_manager = IsolationForestManager.get_instance()

from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils import timezone


class SecurityIncident(models.Model):
    INCIDENT_TYPES = [
        ("BRUTE_FORCE", "Brute Force Authentication Attempt"),
        ("OTP_ABUSE", "Repeated OTP Verification Failure"),
        ("MASS_DOWNLOAD", "Mass / Burst File Download Anomaly"),
        ("INTEGRITY_VIOLATION", "Cryptographic Integrity Verification Failure"),
        ("MALWARE_DETECTED", "Malicious Payload Detected by Antivirus"),
        ("REVOKED_EXPIRED_ACCESS", "Access to Revoked / Expired Resource"),
        ("UNAUTHORIZED_ACCESS", "Unauthorized Resource Access Attempt"),
        ("SUSPICIOUS_SHARING", "Anomalous File Sharing Activity"),
        ("IP_DEVICE_ANOMALY", "Unusual IP / Device Origin Switch"),
        ("ISOLATION_FOREST_ANOMALY", "Behavioral Isolation Forest Outlier"),
    ]

    SEVERITY_LEVELS = [
        ("LOW", "Low"),
        ("MEDIUM", "Medium"),
        ("HIGH", "High"),
        ("CRITICAL", "Critical"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("OPEN", "Open"),
        ("INVESTIGATING", "Investigating"),
        ("RESOLVED", "Resolved"),
        ("FALSE_POSITIVE", "False Positive"),
    ]

    user = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="security_incidents",
    )

    incident_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=64,
        choices=INCIDENT_TYPES,
        db_index=True,
    )

    severity = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=SEVERITY_LEVELS,
        default="MEDIUM",
        db_index=True,
    )

    risk_score = models.SmallIntegerField(
        default=0,
        help_text="Calculated composite risk score (0-100)",
    )

    anomaly_score = models.FloatField(
        default=0.0,
        help_text="Isolation Forest anomaly score (0.0 to 1.0)",
    )

    rule_score = models.FloatField(
        default=0.0,
        help_text="Deterministic rule-based score (0.0 to 100.0)",
    )

    description = models.TextField()

    evidence = models.JSONField(
        default=dict,
        blank=True,
        help_text="Structured evidence including triggered rules, reasons, and feature values",
    )

    source_event = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.AuditLog",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="incidents",
    )

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default="OPEN",
        db_index=True,
    )

    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    user_agent = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        null=True,
    )

    device_information = models.JSONField(
        default=dict,
        blank=True,
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now_add=True,
        db_index=True,
    )

    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(
        auto_now=True,
    )

    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(
        null=True,
        blank=True,
    )

    resolved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="resolved_incidents",
    )

    resolution_notes = models.TextField(
        blank=True,
        default="",
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]
        verbose_name = "Security Incident"
        verbose_name_plural = "Security Incidents"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.severity}] {self.get_incident_type_display()} - User: {self.user or 'Anonymous'} (Score: {self.risk_score})"

    def mark_investigating(self, admin_user=None):
        self.status = "INVESTIGATING"
        self.save(update_fields=["status", "updated_at"])

    def resolve(self, admin_user, notes=""):
        self.status = "RESOLVED"
        self.resolved_by = admin_user
        self.resolved_at = timezone.now()
        self.resolution_notes = notes
        self.save(update_fields=["status", "resolved_by", "resolved_at", "resolution_notes", "updated_at"])

    def mark_false_positive(self, admin_user, notes=""):
        self.status = "FALSE_POSITIVE"
        self.resolved_by = admin_user
        self.resolved_at = timezone.now()
        self.resolution_notes = notes
        self.save(update_fields=["status", "resolved_by", "resolved_at", "resolution_notes", "updated_at"])

FILE: security_ai\rules.py

from .config import RULE_THRESHOLDS
from .baseline import is_download_rate_abnormal


def evaluate_rules(audit_log, features, user=None):
    """
    Evaluates deterministic security rules on the current audit log and extracted feature state.
    
    Returns:
        dict: {
            "rule_score": float (0.0 to 100.0),
            "triggered_rules": list of dict,
            "reasons": list of str,
            "incident_type": str or None,
            "max_rule_severity": str ("LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH", "CRITICAL")
        }
    """
    triggered_rules = []
    reasons = []
    action = (audit_log.action or "").upper()
    event_type = (audit_log.event_type or "").upper()
    description = (audit_log.description or "").lower()

    # 1. Malware Detection (CRITICAL)
    if "MALWARE" in action or "MALWARE" in event_type or "malware" in description:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "MALWARE_DETECTED",
            "name": "Antivirus Malware Detection",
            "severity": "CRITICAL",
            "score": 95.0,
            "reason": "ClamAV detected malicious payload or virus in uploaded file.",
        })
        reasons.append("ClamAV detected a malicious payload in the uploaded file.")

    # 2. Cryptographic Integrity Failure (CRITICAL)
    if "INTEGRITY_FAILED" in action or "AES-GCM" in audit_log.description or "integrity" in description and not audit_log.success:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "INTEGRITY_VIOLATION",
            "name": "Cryptographic Integrity Failure",
            "severity": "CRITICAL",
            "score": 90.0,
            "reason": "Cryptographic verification failed (SHA-256 hash or AES-GCM authentication tag mismatch). Possible tampering or corruption.",
        })
        reasons.append("Cryptographic integrity verification failed (SHA-256 checksum or AES-GCM tag mismatch).")

    # 3. Brute Force Authentication (HIGH)
    failed_logins = int(features.get("failed_logins_1h", 0))
    if ("LOGIN_FAILED" in action or not audit_log.success and "LOGIN" in action) and failed_logins >= RULE_THRESHOLDS["BRUTE_FORCE_FAILED_LOGINS"]:
        score = min(95.0, 60.0 + (failed_logins * 5.0))
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "BRUTE_FORCE_LOGIN",
            "name": "Brute Force Authentication",
            "severity": "HIGH",
            "score": score,
            "reason": f"{failed_logins} failed login attempts detected within the activity window.",
        })
        reasons.append(f"{failed_logins} failed login attempts detected in a short period.")
    elif failed_logins >= RULE_THRESHOLDS["BRUTE_FORCE_FAILED_LOGINS"]:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "BRUTE_FORCE_LOGIN",
            "name": "Brute Force Authentication",
            "severity": "HIGH",
            "score": 75.0,
            "reason": f"High frequency of failed logins ({failed_logins} failures recently).",
        })
        reasons.append(f"{failed_logins} failed login attempts recorded recently.")

    # 4. Repeated OTP Failures (HIGH)
    failed_otps = int(features.get("failed_otps_1h", 0))
    if ("OTP_FAILED" in action or "OTP" in action and not audit_log.success) and failed_otps >= RULE_THRESHOLDS["FAILED_OTP_THRESHOLD"]:
        score = min(90.0, 65.0 + (failed_otps * 8.0))
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "OTP_ABUSE",
            "name": "Repeated OTP Verification Failures",
            "severity": "HIGH",
            "score": score,
            "reason": f"{failed_otps} failed one-time passcode (OTP) verification attempts.",
        })
        reasons.append(f"{failed_otps} failed OTP attempts recorded in a short period.")

    # 5. Mass / Burst File Downloads (HIGH)
    downloads_1h = features.get("downloads_1h", 0.0)
    if "DOWNLOAD" in action:
        is_abnormal, dl_reason, baseline = is_download_rate_abnormal(user, downloads_1h)
        if is_abnormal:
            triggered_rules.append({
                "rule_id": "MASS_DOWNLOAD",
                "name": "Abnormal File Download Volume",
                "severity": "HIGH",
                "score": 75.0,
                "reason": dl_reason,
            })
            reasons.append(dl_reason)

    # 6. Access to Revoked or Expired Links (HIGH / MEDIUM)
    if "REVOKED" in action or "revoked" in description:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "REVOKED_ACCESS",
            "name": "Access Attempt on Revoked Share",
            "severity": "HIGH",
            "score": 70.0,
            "reason": "Attempted to access or download a revoked share link.",
        })
        reasons.append("Unauthorized attempt to access a revoked file share link.")
    elif "EXPIRED" in action or "expired" in description:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "EXPIRED_ACCESS",
            "name": "Access Attempt on Expired Share",
            "severity": "MEDIUM",
            "score": 50.0,
            "reason": "Attempted to access an expired share link.",
        })
        reasons.append("Attempt to access an expired file share link.")

    # 7. Share Password Failures (MEDIUM)
    if "SHARE_PASSWORD_FAILED" in action or ("password" in description and "incorrect" in description):
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "SHARE_PASSWORD_FAILED",
            "name": "Incorrect Share Password",
            "severity": "MEDIUM",
            "score": 45.0,
            "reason": "Incorrect password entered for protected share link.",
        })
        reasons.append("Incorrect password submitted for protected shared file.")

    # 8. Distinct IP Origin Anomaly (MEDIUM)
    distinct_ips = int(features.get("distinct_ips_24h", 1))
    if distinct_ips >= RULE_THRESHOLDS["DISTINCT_IPS_1H_THRESHOLD"]:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "MULTI_IP_ANOMALY",
            "name": "Multiple IP Addresses Detected",
            "severity": "MEDIUM",
            "score": 40.0,
            "reason": f"Activity from {distinct_ips} different IP addresses within 24 hours.",
        })
        reasons.append(f"Account active from {distinct_ips} distinct IP addresses within 24 hours.")

    # 9. Burst Sharing Activity (MEDIUM)
    shares_1h = int(features.get("shares_1h", 0))
    if "SHARE" in action and shares_1h >= RULE_THRESHOLDS["BURST_SHARING_COUNT"]:
        triggered_rules.append({
            "rule_id": "BURST_SHARING",
            "name": "High Volume Sharing Spike",
            "severity": "MEDIUM",
            "score": 50.0,
            "reason": f"Unusually high file sharing activity ({shares_1h} files shared in 1 hour).",
        })
        reasons.append(f"High-frequency file sharing: {shares_1h} files shared in the last hour.")

    # Calculate aggregate rule score (highest rule score + fractional contribution from others)
    if not triggered_rules:
        return {
            "rule_score": 0.0,
            "triggered_rules": [],
            "reasons": [],
            "incident_type": None,
            "max_rule_severity": "LOW",
        }

    # Sort rules descending by score
    sorted_rules = sorted(triggered_rules, key=lambda r: r["score"], reverse=True)
    top_rule = sorted_rules[0]

    # Diminishing returns sum for multiple rules
    combined_score = top_rule["score"]
    for r in sorted_rules[1:]:
        combined_score += (100.0 - combined_score) * (r["score"] / 200.0)

    combined_score = min(100.0, round(combined_score, 1))

    # Incident type mapping
    incident_type_map = {
        "MALWARE_DETECTED": "MALWARE_DETECTED",
        "INTEGRITY_VIOLATION": "INTEGRITY_VIOLATION",
        "BRUTE_FORCE_LOGIN": "BRUTE_FORCE",
        "OTP_ABUSE": "OTP_ABUSE",
        "MASS_DOWNLOAD": "MASS_DOWNLOAD",
        "REVOKED_ACCESS": "REVOKED_EXPIRED_ACCESS",
        "EXPIRED_ACCESS": "REVOKED_EXPIRED_ACCESS",
        "SHARE_PASSWORD_FAILED": "UNAUTHORIZED_ACCESS",
        "MULTI_IP_ANOMALY": "IP_DEVICE_ANOMALY",
        "BURST_SHARING": "SUSPICIOUS_SHARING",
    }

    return {
        "rule_score": combined_score,
        "triggered_rules": triggered_rules,
        "reasons": reasons,
        "incident_type": incident_type_map.get(
        top_rule["rule_id"],
        "ISOLATION_FOREST_ANOMALY",),
        "max_rule_severity": top_rule["severity"],
    }

from django.urls import path
from . import views

app_name = "security_ai"

urlpatterns = [
    path(
        "dashboard/",
        views.security_dashboard,
        name="security_dashboard",
    ),

    path(
        "incident/<int:incident_id>/",
        views.incident_detail,
        name="incident_detail",
    ),
]

======================================================================
FILE: security_ai\views.py
======================================================================
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, render

from .models import SecurityIncident


@login_required(login_url="login")
def security_dashboard(request):

    incidents = (
        SecurityIncident.objects
        .filter(user=request.user)
        .select_related("user")
        .order_by("-created_at")
    )

    context = {
        "incidents": incidents[:100],
        "total_incidents": incidents.count(),
        "critical_count": incidents.filter(
            severity="CRITICAL"
        ).count(),
        "high_count": incidents.filter(
            severity="HIGH"
        ).count(),
        "medium_count": incidents.filter(
            severity="MEDIUM"
        ).count(),
        "open_count": incidents.filter(
            status="OPEN"
        ).count(),
    }

    return render(
        request,
        "security_ai/dashboard.html",
        context,
    )


@login_required(login_url="login")
def incident_detail(request, incident_id):

    incident = get_object_or_404(
        SecurityIncident.objects.select_related(
            "user",
            "source_event"
        ),
        id=incident_id,
        user=request.user,
    )

    return render(
        request,
        "security_ai/incident_detail.html",
        {
            "incident": incident
        },
    )

FILE: test_incident_dedup.py
======================================================================
from django.db import transaction
from accounts.models import AuditLog
from security_ai.engine import create_or_update_incident
from security_ai.models import SecurityIncident

before_logs = AuditLog.objects.count()
before_incidents = SecurityIncident.objects.count()
test_ip = "192.0.2.77"

try:
    with transaction.atomic():
        first_log = AuditLog.objects.create(
            user=None,
            action="MALWARE_DETECTED",
            description="Deduplication test event 1",
            ip_address=test_ip,
            user_agent="IntelliShare-Dedup-Test",
            metadata={},
            success=False,
            event_type="SECURITY",
        )

        first = create_or_update_incident(
            audit_log=first_log,
            incident_type="MALWARE_DETECTED",
            severity="CRITICAL",
            risk_score=95,
            anomaly_score=0.8,
            rule_score=95,
            reasons=["Test malware rule"],
            features={},
        )

        second_log = AuditLog.objects.create(
            user=None,
            action="MALWARE_DETECTED",
            description="Deduplication test event 2",
            ip_address=test_ip,
            user_agent="IntelliShare-Dedup-Test",
            metadata={},
            success=False,
            event_type="SECURITY",
        )

        second = create_or_update_incident(
            audit_log=second_log,
            incident_type="MALWARE_DETECTED",
            severity="CRITICAL",
            risk_score=98,
            anomaly_score=0.9,
            rule_score=95,
            reasons=["Repeated test malware rule"],
            features={},
        )

        print("Same incident ID:", first.pk == second.pk)
        print("First incident ID:", first.pk)
        print("Second incident ID:", second.pk)
        print("Updated risk score:", second.risk_score)
        print("Correlation count:", second.evidence.get("correlation_count"))
        print("Merged reasons:", second.evidence.get("reasons"))

        transaction.set_rollback(True)
except Exception as exc:
    print("Test error:", type(exc).__name__, str(exc))

print("Audit logs before/after:", before_logs, AuditLog.objects.count())
print("Incidents before/after:", before_incidents, SecurityIncident.objects.count())

FILE: test_multi_ip_rule.py

from security_ai.rules import evaluate_rules
from types import SimpleNamespace

log = SimpleNamespace(
    action="LOGIN_SUCCESS",
    event_type="AUTHENTICATION",
    description="",
    success=True
)

for n in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    result = evaluate_rules(log, {"distinct_ips_24h": n})
    triggered = any(
        rule.get("rule_id") == "MULTI_IP_ANOMALY"
        for rule in result["triggered_rules"]
    )
    print(f"{n} IPs: triggered={triggered}")

FILE: test_security_engine.py

from django.db import transaction
from accounts.models import AuditLog
from security_ai.engine import evaluate_security_event
from security_ai.models import SecurityIncident

before_logs = AuditLog.objects.count()
before_incidents = SecurityIncident.objects.count()

try:
    with transaction.atomic():
        log = AuditLog.objects.create(
            user=None,
            action="MALWARE_DETECTED",
            description="Test malware detection",
            ip_address="192.0.2.10",
            user_agent="IntelliShare-Test",
            metadata={},
            success=False,
            event_type="SECURITY",
        )

        result = evaluate_security_event(log)

        print("Risk score:", result["risk_score"])
        print("Risk level:", result["risk_level"])
        print("Suspicious:", result["suspicious"])
        print("Reasons:", result["reasons"])
        print("Incident created:", result["incident"] is not None)

        if result["incident"]:
            print("Incident type:", result["incident"].incident_type)
            print("Incident severity:", result["incident"].severity)

        transaction.set_rollback(True)
except Exception as exc:
    print("Test error:", type(exc).__name__, str(exc))

print("Audit logs before/after:", before_logs, AuditLog.objects.count())
print("Incidents before/after:", before_incidents, SecurityIncident.objects.count())


======================================================================
FILE: test_share_access_rules.py
======================================================================
from security_ai.rules import evaluate_rules
from types import SimpleNamespace

cases = [
    ("REVOKED_ACCESS", "DOWNLOAD_REVOKED", "AUTHENTICATION"),
    ("EXPIRED_ACCESS", "DOWNLOAD_EXPIRED", "AUTHENTICATION"),
    ("NORMAL_ACCESS", "DOWNLOAD_SUCCESS", "AUTHENTICATION"),
]

for name, action, event_type in cases:
    log = SimpleNamespace(
        action=action,
        event_type=event_type,
        description="",
        success=True
    )
    result = evaluate_rules(log, {})
    matches = [
        rule for rule in result["triggered_rules"]
        if rule.get("rule_id") in ["REVOKED_ACCESS", "EXPIRED_ACCESS"]
    ]
    print(f"{name}: triggered={bool(matches)}, rules={matches}")


======================================================================
FILE: test_sharing_rules.py
======================================================================
from security_ai.rules import evaluate_rules
from types import SimpleNamespace

def make_log(action, description=""):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        action=action,
        event_type="SHARING",
        description=description,
        success=False
    )

tests = [
    ("Wrong share password", make_log("SHARE_PASSWORD_FAILED"), {}),
    ("Incorrect password description", make_log("OTHER", "Incorrect password entered"), {}),
    ("Normal password event", make_log("SHARE_PASSWORD_SUCCESS"), {}),
    ("9 shares", make_log("SHARE_FILE"), {"shares_1h": 9}),
    ("10 shares", make_log("SHARE_FILE"), {"shares_1h": 10}),
    ("12 shares", make_log("SHARE_FILE"), {"shares_1h": 12}),
]

for name, log, features in tests:
    result = evaluate_rules(log, features)
    ids = [rule["rule_id"] for rule in result["triggered_rules"]]
    print(f"{name}: {ids}")
